Finally, embedded delivery to an ARM Cortex-M7 - representative of commercial off-the-shelf devices used in New Space platforms - confirms the real-time feasibility of these approaches and highlights their suitability for onboard attitude control in resource-constrained spacecraft rendezvous missions.

</details>


### [46] [PRIOR: Perceptive Learning for Humanoid Locomotion with Reference Gait Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18979)
*Chenxi Han,Shilu He,Yi Cheng,Linqi Ye,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIOR是一个高效可复现的人形机器人运动框架，通过参数化步态生成器、基于GRU的地形状态估计器和地形自适应脚步奖励，实现了在复杂地形上的鲁棒穿越和类人步态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练感知型人形机器人运动策略在复杂地形上实现自然步态仍面临挑战，通常需要多阶段训练流程、对抗性目标或大量真实世界校准。需要一种更高效、可复现的解决方案。

Method: 1. 参数化步态生成器：提供基于运动捕捉的稳定参考轨迹，无需对抗训练；2. GRU状态估计器：通过自监督高度图重建从第一人称深度图像推断地形几何；3. 地形自适应脚步奖励：引导脚步放置到可穿越区域；4. 系统分析深度图像分辨率权衡，在实时约束下最大化地形保真度。

Result: 在包括楼梯、箱子和间隙等不同难度地形上的综合实验表明，每个组件都带来互补且必要的性能提升，完整框架实现了100%的穿越成功率。深度图像分辨率优化显著减少了感知开销而不降低穿越性能。

Conclusion: PRIOR框架通过简单而有效的设计实现了鲁棒的地形穿越和类人步态，将开源完整的训练流程、参数化步态生成器和评估基准，为Isaac Lab上的人形机器人运动研究提供可复现的基础。

Abstract: Training perceptive humanoid locomotion policies that traverse complex terrains with natural gaits remains an open challenge, typically demanding multi-stage training pipelines, adversarial objectives, or extensive real-world calibration. We present PRIOR, an efficient and reproducible framework built on Isaac Lab that achieves robust terrain traversal with human-like gaits through a simple yet effective design: (i) a parametric gait generator that supplies stable reference trajectories derived from motion capture without adversarial training, (ii) a GRU-based state estimator that infers terrain geometry directly from egocentric depth images via self-supervised heightmap reconstruction, and (iii) terrain-adaptive footstep rewards that guide foot placement toward traversable regions. Through systematic analysis of depth image resolution trade-offs, we identify configurations that maximize terrain fidelity under real-time constraints, substantially reducing perceptual overhead without degrading traversal performance. Comprehensive experiments across terrains of varying difficulty-including stairs, boxes, and gaps-demonstrate that each component yields complementary and essential performance gains, with the full framework achieving a 100% traversal success rate. We will open-source the complete PRIOR framework, including the training pipeline, parametric gait generator, and evaluation benchmarks, to serve as a reproducible foundation for humanoid locomotion research on Isaac Lab.

</details>


### [47] [MERGE: Guided Vision-Language Models for Multi-Actor Event Reasoning and Grounding in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18988)
*Joerg Deigmoeller,Nakul Agarwal,Stephan Hasler,Daniel Tanneberg,Anna Belardinelli,Reza Ghoddoosian,Chao Wang,Felix Ocker,Fan Zhang,Behzad Dariush,Michael Gienger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MERGE系统通过视觉语言模型与感知流水线结合，实现动态人机群体交互中参与者、物体和事件的情境化定位，提高定位准确率2倍并减少4倍运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人机群体交互需要一致的情境感知，但现有方法存在高成本、高延迟和碎片化输出问题，且缺乏合适的多参与者协作基准数据集。

Method: MERGE系统采用解耦设计：轻量级流式模块持续处理视觉输入检测变化，仅在必要时调用视觉语言模型；通过唯一识别物理实例并构建参与者-动作-对象关系确保时间一致性。

Result: 在GROUND数据集上，MERGE相比纯VLM基线（包括GPT-4o、GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash）将平均定位分数提高2倍，运行时间减少4倍。

Conclusion: MERGE通过解耦感知与推理的设计，在保持VLM零样本泛化能力的同时显著提升效率和准确性，为多参与者协作情境理解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce MERGE, a system for situational grounding of actors, objects, and events in dynamic human-robot group interactions. Effective collaboration in such settings requires consistent situational awareness, built on persistent representations of people and objects and an episodic abstraction of events. MERGE achieves this by uniquely identifying physical instances of actors (humans or robots) and objects and structuring them into actor-action-object relations, ensuring temporal consistency across interactions. Central to MERGE is the integration of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) guided with a perception pipeline: a lightweight streaming module continuously processes visual input to detect changes and selectively invokes the VLM only when necessary. This decoupled design preserves the reasoning power and zero-shot generalization of VLMs while improving efficiency, avoiding both the high monetary cost and the latency of frame-by-frame captioning that leads to fragmented and delayed outputs. To address the absence of suitable benchmarks for multi-actor collaboration, we introduce the GROUND dataset, which offers fine-grained situational annotations of multi-person and human-robot interactions. On this dataset, our approach improves the average grounding score by a factor of 2 compared to the performance of VLM-only baselines - including GPT-4o, GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Flash - while also reducing run-time by a factor of 4. The code and data are available at www.github.com/HRI-EU/merge.

</details>


### [48] [ATG-MoE: Autoregressive trajectory generation with mixture-of-experts for assembly skill learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19029)
*Weihang Huang,Chaoran Zhang,Xiaoxin Deng,Hao Zhou,Zhaobo Xu,Shubo Cui,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATG-MoE：一种用于装配技能学习的端到端自回归轨迹生成方法，通过专家混合架构实现多模态输入到操作轨迹的闭环映射，支持高效的多技能集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程劳动密集且不灵活，现有基于学习的装配方法存在位置泛化能力弱、多阶段设计复杂、多技能集成能力有限等问题。需要一种能够适应不断变化任务、物体和环境的灵活制造系统。

Method: 提出ATG-MoE方法，建立从RGB-D观测、自然语言指令和机器人本体感知等多模态输入到操作轨迹的闭环映射。集成多模态特征融合用于场景和任务理解，自回归序列建模用于时间一致的轨迹生成，专家混合架构用于统一的多技能学习。

Result: 在减压阀装配任务的八个代表性装配技能上进行训练和评估。仿真实验显示平均抓取成功率96.3%，平均总体成功率91.8%，表现出强大的泛化能力和有效的多技能集成。真实世界实验进一步验证了其多技能工业装配的实用性。

Conclusion: ATG-MoE通过端到端自回归轨迹生成和专家混合架构，解决了传统方法在位置泛化、多阶段设计和多技能集成方面的局限性，为灵活制造提供了有效的机器人装配技能学习方法。

Abstract: Flexible manufacturing requires robot systems that can adapt to constantly changing tasks, objects, and environments. However, traditional robot programming is labor-intensive and inflexible, while existing learning-based assembly methods often suffer from weak positional generalization, complex multi-stage designs, and limited multi-skill integration capability. To address these issues, this paper proposes ATG-MoE, an end-to-end autoregressive trajectory generation method with mixture of experts for assembly skill learning from demonstration. The proposed method establishes a closed-loop mapping from multi-modal inputs, including RGB-D observations, natural language instructions, and robot proprioception to manipulation trajectories. It integrates multi-modal feature fusion for scene and task understanding, autoregressive sequence modeling for temporally coherent trajectory generation, and a mixture-of-experts architecture for unified multi-skill learning. In contrast to conventional methods that separate visual perception and control or train different skills independently, ATG-MoE directly incorporates visual information into trajectory generation and supports efficient multi-skill integration within a single model. We train and evaluate the proposed method on eight representative assembly skills from a pressure-reducing valve assembly task. Experimental results show that ATG-MoE achieves strong overall performance in simulation, with an average grasp success rate of 96.3% and an average overall success rate of 91.8%, while also demonstrating strong generalization and effective multi-skill integration. Real-world experiments further verify its practicality for multi-skill industrial assembly. The project page can be found at https://hwh23.github.io/ATG-MoE

</details>


### [49] [Fire as a Service: Augmenting Robot Simulators with Thermally and Visually Accurate Fire Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19063)
*Anton R. Wagner,Madhan Balaji Rao,Helge Wrede,Sören Pirk,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FaaS是一个异步协同仿真框架，为现有机器人模拟器添加高保真火灾模拟，支持热力学热传递和烟雾效果，用于消防机器人能力评估和训练数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人模拟器主要关注刚体动力学和逼真渲染，但忽略了真实火灾环境中的热学和光学复杂现象，这限制了消防机器人的可靠能力评估和训练数据生成。

Method: 提出Fire as a Service (FaaS)异步协同仿真框架，将高保真、计算高效的火灾模拟与现有机器人模拟器集成，支持多物种热力学热传递和视觉一致的体积烟雾，同时不干扰高频刚体控制循环。

Result: FaaS能够生成物理精确的火灾行为，评估机器人平台面临的热危害，收集真实的多模态感知数据，支持实时人机交互遥操作，并通过行为克隆成功训练反应式多模态策略。

Conclusion: 通过为机器人模拟添加火灾动力学，FaaS为在火灾场景中更安全、更可靠的机器人部署提供了可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Most existing robot simulators prioritize rigid-body dynamics and photorealistic rendering, but largely neglect the thermally and optically complex phenomena that characterize real-world fire environments. For robots envisioned as future firefighters, this limitation hinders both reliable capability evaluation and the generation of representative training data prior to deployment in hazardous scenarios. To address these challenges, we introduce Fire as a Service (FaaS), a novel, asynchronous co-simulation framework that augments existing robot simulators with high-fidelity and computationally efficient fire simulations. Our pipeline enables robots to experience accurate, multi-species thermodynamic heat transfer and visually consistent volumetric smoke without disrupting high-frequency rigid-body control loops. We demonstrate that our framework can be integrated with diverse robot simulators to generate physically accurate fire behavior, benchmark thermal hazards encountered by robotic platforms, and collect realistic multimodal perceptual data. Crucially, its real-time performance supports human-in-the-loop teleoperation, enabling the successful training of reactive, multimodal policies via Behavioral Cloning. By adding fire dynamics to robot simulations, FaaS provides a scalable pathway toward safer, more reliable deployment of robots in fire scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [CAMO: A Conditional Neural Solver for the Multi-objective Multiple Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19074)
*Fengxiaoxiao Li,Xiao Mao,Mingfeng Fan,Yifeng Zhang,Yi Li,Tanishq Duhan,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAMO是一个用于多目标多旅行商问题的条件神经求解器，能够泛化到不同目标数、智能体数和偏好向量，生成高质量的帕累托前沿近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需要团队协作访问多个目标并优化竞争性目标（如总旅行成本和完工时间），这被建模为多目标多旅行商问题。现有学习方法主要针对单智能体TSP和多目标TSP变体，很少同时处理多智能体协调和多目标权衡的双重复杂性挑战。

Method: CAMO包含条件编码器（将偏好向量融入实例表示以显式控制多目标权衡）和协作解码器（通过交替智能体选择和节点选择自回归构建多智能体路径）。使用基于REINFORCE的目标在混合问题规模分布上进行训练以提高泛化能力。

Result: CAMO在实验中优于神经和传统启发式方法，实现了更接近帕累托前沿的近似解。消融研究验证了关键组件的贡献，在移动机器人平台上的实际测试证明了其实用性。

Conclusion: CAMO成功解决了多目标多旅行商问题的双重复杂性，通过条件神经架构实现了对多目标权衡的显式控制和多智能体协调，具有良好的泛化能力和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Robotic systems often require a team of robots to collectively visit multiple targets while optimizing competing objectives, such as total travel cost and makespan. This setting can be formulated as the Multi-Objective Multiple Traveling Salesman Problem (MOMTSP). Although learning-based methods have shown strong performance on the single-agent TSP and multi-objective TSP variants, they rarely address the combined challenges of multi-agent coordination and multi-objective trade-offs, which introduce dual sources of complexity. To bridge this gap, we propose CAMO, a conditional neural solver for MOMTSP that generalizes across varying numbers of targets, agents, and preference vectors, and yields high-quality approximations to the Pareto front (PF). Specifically, CAMO consists of a conditional encoder to fuse preferences into instance representations, enabling explicit control over multi-objective trade-offs, and a collaborative decoder that coordinates all agents by alternating agent selection and node selection to construct multi-agent tours autoregressively. To further improve generalization, we train CAMO with a REINFORCE-based objective over a mixed distribution of problem sizes. Extensive experiments show that CAMO outperforms both neural and conventional heuristics, achieving a closer approximation of PFs. In addition, ablation results validate the contributions of CAMO's key components, and real-world tests on a mobile robot platform demonstrate its practical applicability.

</details>


### [51] [Tendon-Actuated Robots with a Tapered, Flexible Polymer Backbone: Design, Fabrication, and Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19124)
*Harald Minde Hansen,Nandita Gallacher,Nicholas B. Andrews,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Jan Tommy Gravdahl,Mario di Castro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D打印、肌腱驱动的锥形连续体机器人设计框架，采用TPU柔性锥形骨架，集成电子基座实现肌腱张力控制与传感，建立了基于Cosserat杆理论的广义运动静力学模型，并通过运动捕捉验证了模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人多为单用途、高成本设计，缺乏可定制性、快速组装和低成本特性。本文旨在开发一种可定制、快速组装、低成本的锥形连续体机器人，通过几何锥形化实现高曲率和远端柔顺性，支持广泛的柔顺机器人检测与操作任务。

Method: 1) 设计基于TPU的柔性锥形骨架，采用熔融沉积成型3D打印制造；2) 开发集成电子基座，包含执行器和压缩载荷传感器实现肌腱张力控制与传感；3) 基于Cosserat杆理论建立广义运动静力学模型，采用牛顿方法，扩展现有肌腱驱动Cosserat杆公式以显式考虑空间变化的骨架截面几何；4) 通过参数化iLogic/CAD脚本实现快速几何生成与缩放；5) 使用运动捕捉系统验证模型精度，通过线搜索校准杨氏模量最小化建模误差。

Result: 1) 成功制造出低成本、可定制的锥形连续体机器人；2) 建立的广义运动静力学模型能够准确捕捉锥形引起的梯度刚度分布；3) 模型验证显示厘米级形状预测精度；4) 分析了骨架锥角对机器人配置空间和可操作性的影响；5) 演示了通过6自由度机械臂搭载内窥镜夹持器的遥操作抓取；6) 提供了从参数化设计到控制肌腱驱动的完整可重复框架。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个从参数化设计到控制肌腱驱动的简单、快速、可重复框架，用于制造基于熔融沉积成型3D打印的锥形肌腱驱动连续体机器人。该设计优先考虑可定制性、快速组装和低成本，同时通过几何锥形实现高曲率和增强的远端柔顺性，支持广泛的柔顺机器人应用。建立的模型为系统探索几何设计参数对配置空间的影响提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, modeling, and fabrication of 3D-printed, tendon-actuated continuum robots featuring a flexible, tapered backbone constructed from thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). Our scalable design incorporates an integrated electronics base housing that enables direct tendon tension control and sensing via actuators and compression load cells. Unlike many continuum robots that are single-purpose and costly, the proposed design prioritizes customizability, rapid assembly, and low cost while enabling high curvature and enhanced distal compliance through geometric tapering, thereby supporting a broad range of compliant robotic inspection and manipulation tasks. We develop a generalized forward kinetostatic model of the tapered backbone based on Cosserat rod theory using a Newtonian approach, extending existing tendon-actuated Cosserat rod formulations to explicitly account for spatially varying backbone cross-sectional geometry. The model captures the graded stiffness profile induced by the tapering and enables systematic exploration of the configuration space as a function of the geometric design parameters. Specifically, we analyze how the backbone taper angle influences the robot's configuration space and manipulability. The model is validated against motion capture data, achieving centimeter-level shape prediction accuracy after calibrating Young's modulus via a line search that minimizes modeling error. We further demonstrate teleoperated grasping using an endoscopic gripper routed along the continuum robot, mounted on a 6-DoF robotic arm. Parameterized iLogic/CAD scripts are provided for rapid geometry generation and scaling. The presented framework establishes a simple, rapid, and reproducible pathway from parametric design to controlled tendon actuation for tapered, tendon-driven continuum robots manufactured using fused deposition modeling 3D printers.

</details>


### [52] [Introducing M: A Modular, Modifiable Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19134)
*Victor Nikhil Antony,Zhili Gong,Yoonjae Kim,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M是一个开源、低成本的社会机器人平台，旨在通过使机器人更易于复制、修改和部署来减少社会机器人研究中的平台摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会机器人研究中存在平台摩擦问题，包括机器人难以复制、修改和在真实环境中部署，这阻碍了研究的进展和可重复性。

Method: M平台采用模块化机械设计、多模态传感和表达性但机械简单的驱动架构，结合ROS2原生软件包，清晰分离感知、表达控制和数据管理。平台包含仿真环境，支持交互行为的快速仿真到真实环境迁移。

Result: 平台展示了通过额外传感/驱动模块的可扩展性，提供了讲故事和双向对话辅导的交互模板示例。在实际应用中，M成功用于参与式设计和为期一周的家庭部署，验证了其作为纵向、可重复社会机器人研究实用基础的能力。

Conclusion: M平台通过降低平台摩擦，为社会机器人研究提供了实用、可扩展的基础，支持纵向、可重复的研究，促进了社会机器人领域的进展。

Abstract: We present M, an open-source, low-cost social robot platform designed to reduce platform friction that slows social robotics research by making robots easier to reproduce, modify, and deploy in real-world settings. M combines a modular mechanical design, multimodal sensing, and expressive yet mechanically simple actuation architecture with a ROS2-native software package that cleanly separates perception, expression control, and data management. The platform includes a simulation environment with interface equivalence to hardware to support rapid sim-to-real transfer of interaction behaviors. We demonstrate extensibility through additional sensing/actuation modules and provide example interaction templates for storytelling and two-way conversational coaching. Finally, we report real-world use in participatory design and week-long in-home deployments, showing how M can serve as a practical foundation for longitudinal, reproducible social robotics research.

</details>


### [53] [Meanings and Measurements: Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19166)
*Swagat Padhan,Lakshya Jain,Bhavya Minesh Shah,Omkar Patil,Thao Nguyen,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPG：多智能体概率式语义-度量联合接地框架，通过分解语言查询并概率组合VLM输出，解决复杂度量语义语言指令的3D空间接地问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）在语义接地方面表现良好，但缺乏对物理空间中度量约束的显式推理能力，难以处理包含语义参考、空间关系和度量约束的复杂语言查询

Method: 提出MAPG多智能体概率式接地框架：1）将语言查询分解为结构化子组件；2）查询VLM对每个组件进行接地；3）概率组合这些接地输出，在3D空间中生成度量一致的可执行决策

Result: 在HM-EQA基准测试中性能持续优于强基线；提出新基准MAPG-Bench专门评估度量语义目标接地；真实机器人演示表明MAPG在结构化场景表示可用时可迁移到现实世界

Conclusion: MAPG通过分解和概率组合策略有效解决了复杂度量语义语言查询的3D空间接地问题，填补了现有语言接地评估的空白，并展示了从仿真到真实世界的可迁移性

Abstract: Robots collaborating with humans must convert natural language goals into actionable, physically grounded decisions. For example, executing a command such as "go two meters to the right of the fridge" requires grounding semantic references, spatial relations, and metric constraints within a 3D scene. While recent vision language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong semantic grounding capabilities, they are not explicitly designed to reason about metric constraints in physically defined spaces. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that state-of-the-art VLM-based grounding approaches struggle with complex metric-semantic language queries. To address this limitation, we propose MAPG (Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding), an agentic framework that decomposes language queries into structured subcomponents and queries a VLM to ground each component. MAPG then probabilistically composes these grounded outputs to produce metrically consistent, actionable decisions in 3D space. We evaluate MAPG on the HM-EQA benchmark and show consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a new benchmark, MAPG-Bench, specifically designed to evaluate metric-semantic goal grounding, addressing a gap in existing language grounding evaluations. We also present a real-world robot demonstration showing that MAPG transfers beyond simulation when a structured scene representation is available.

</details>


### [54] [Sparse Autoencoders Reveal Interpretable and Steerable Features in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19183)
*Aiden Swann,Lachlain McGranahan,Hugo Buurmeijer,Monroe Kennedy,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究应用机制可解释性技术分析VLA模型，通过稀疏自编码器提取特征，发现大部分特征对应训练演示的记忆序列，但少数特征对应可解释、通用、可操控的运动基元和语义属性，为VLA泛化性提供机制证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中表现出不一致的泛化能力：在某些场景表现优异，但在新物体、新场景和新指令上微调版本常失败。需要理解VLA模型内部工作机制以解释这种泛化不一致性。

Method: 应用机制可解释性技术，在VLA模型隐藏层激活上训练稀疏自编码器(SAEs)，提取稀疏字典特征作为模型计算的紧凑可解释基。提出特征分类指标区分通用可迁移基元与特定演示记忆。在LIBERO基准上进行操控实验验证。

Result: 发现大部分SAE特征对应特定训练演示的记忆序列，但少数特征对应可解释、通用、可操控的运动基元和语义属性。个体SAE特征能因果影响机器人行为，操控通用特征可跨任务和场景诱导一致行为。监督微调小数据集会放大记忆，而更大更多样数据集或知识隔离促进更通用特征。

Conclusion: 首次提供机制证据表明VLA模型能跨任务和场景学习通用特征。稀疏自编码器特征分析揭示了VLA泛化能力的内部机制，为改进模型设计提供方向：使用更大更多样数据集和知识隔离技术可促进通用特征学习而非记忆。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for general-purpose robot manipulation. However, their generalization is inconsistent: while these models can perform impressively in some settings, fine-tuned variants often fail on novel objects, scenes, and instructions. We apply mechanistic interpretability techniques to better understand the inner workings of VLA models. To probe internal representations, we train Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) on hidden layer activations of the VLA. SAEs learn a sparse dictionary whose features act as a compact, interpretable basis for the model's computation. We find that the large majority of extracted SAE features correspond to memorized sequences from specific training demonstrations. However, some features correspond to interpretable, general, and steerable motion primitives and semantic properties, offering a promising glimpse toward VLA generalizability. We propose a metric to categorize features according to whether they represent generalizable transferable primitives or episode-specific memorization. We validate these findings through steering experiments on the LIBERO benchmark. We show that individual SAE features causally influence robot behavior. Steering general features induces behaviors consistent with their semantic meaning and can be applied across tasks and scenes. This work provides the first mechanistic evidence that VLAs can learn generalizable features across tasks and scenes. We observe that supervised fine-tuning on small robotics datasets disproportionately amplifies memorization. In contrast, training on larger, more diverse datasets (e.g., DROID) or using knowledge insulation promotes more general features. We provide an open-source codebase and user-friendly interface for activation collection, SAE training, and feature steering. Our project page is located at http://drvla.github.io

</details>


### [55] [FASTER: Rethinking Real-Time Flow VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19199)
*Yuxiang Lu,Zhe Liu,Xianzhe Fan,Zhenya Yang,Jinghua Hou,Junyi Li,Kaixin Ding,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FASTER方法通过引入Horizon-Aware Schedule，在流式VLA模型中优先处理近期动作采样，将即时反应的去噪过程压缩10倍至单步完成，显著降低机器人反应延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步推理方法主要优化轨迹平滑性，但忽视了响应环境变化的关键延迟问题。标准流程中恒定调度方案效率低下，要求系统完成所有采样步骤后才能开始运动，形成反应延迟瓶颈

Method: 提出FASTER方法，包含Horizon-Aware Schedule自适应调度机制，在流采样过程中优先处理近期动作，将即时反应去噪压缩至单步完成；结合流式客户端-服务器流水线部署

Result: 在真实机器人上显著降低有效反应延迟，特别是在消费级GPU上；在高度动态的乒乓球任务等现实实验中，FASTER解锁了通用策略前所未有的实时响应能力，能够快速生成准确平滑的轨迹

Conclusion: 通过重新思考动作分块策略中的反应概念，FASTER解决了VLA模型实时执行的关键延迟问题，为物理世界部署提供了高效的实时响应解决方案

Abstract: Real-time execution is crucial for deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in the physical world. Existing asynchronous inference methods primarily optimize trajectory smoothness, but neglect the critical latency in reacting to environmental changes. By rethinking the notion of reaction in action chunking policies, this paper presents a systematic analysis of the factors governing reaction time. We show that reaction time follows a uniform distribution determined jointly by the Time to First Action (TTFA) and the execution horizon. Moreover, we reveal that the standard practice of applying a constant schedule in flow-based VLAs can be inefficient and forces the system to complete all sampling steps before any movement can start, forming the bottleneck in reaction latency. To overcome this issue, we propose Fast Action Sampling for ImmediaTE Reaction (FASTER). By introducing a Horizon-Aware Schedule, FASTER adaptively prioritizes near-term actions during flow sampling, compressing the denoising of the immediate reaction by tenfold (e.g., in $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA) into a single step, while preserving the quality of long-horizon trajectory. Coupled with a streaming client-server pipeline, FASTER substantially reduces the effective reaction latency on real robots, especially when deployed on consumer-grade GPUs. Real-world experiments, including a highly dynamic table tennis task, prove that FASTER unlocks unprecedented real-time responsiveness for generalist policies, enabling rapid generation of accurate and smooth trajectories.

</details>


### [56] [OmniVTA: Visuo-Tactile World Modeling for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19201)
*Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yujie Zang,Shuai Tian,Xiang Li,Ruihai Wu,Ce Hao,Chen Gao,Si Liu,Haoran Li,Yilun Chen,Shuicheng Yan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniViTac是一个大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集，包含21,000+轨迹、86个任务和100+个物体；OmniVTA是基于世界模型的视觉-触觉操作框架，通过预测接触演化和高频触觉反馈实现接触丰富的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务（如擦拭和装配）需要准确感知接触力、摩擦力变化和状态转换，这些无法仅从视觉可靠推断。现有视觉-触觉操作研究存在两个主要限制：数据集规模小、任务覆盖窄；现有方法将触觉信号视为被动观测而非用于建模接触动力学或实现显式闭环控制。

Method: 提出OmniViTac大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集（21,000+轨迹，86个任务，100+物体，6种物理基础交互模式）。基于此提出OmniVTA框架，包含四个紧密耦合模块：自监督触觉编码器、双流视觉-触觉世界模型（预测短期接触演化）、接触感知融合策略（动作生成）、60Hz反射控制器（闭环校正预测与观测触觉信号偏差）。

Result: 真实机器人实验在所有六种交互类别中显示，OmniVTA优于现有方法，并能良好泛化到未见过的物体和几何配置，证实了将预测性接触建模与高频触觉反馈相结合对于接触丰富操作的价值。

Conclusion: OmniVTA通过整合预测性接触建模和高频触觉反馈，显著提升了接触丰富操作任务的性能，证明了大规模视觉-触觉数据集和基于世界模型的方法在机器人操作中的有效性。所有数据、模型和代码将公开。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation tasks, such as wiping and assembly, require accurate perception of contact forces, friction changes, and state transitions that cannot be reliably inferred from vision alone. Despite growing interest in visuo-tactile manipulation, progress is constrained by two persistent limitations: existing datasets are small in scale and narrow in task coverage, and current methods treat tactile signals as passive observations rather than using them to model contact dynamics or enable closed-loop control explicitly. In this paper, we present \textbf{OmniViTac}, a large-scale visuo-tactile-action dataset comprising $21{,}000+$ trajectories across $86$ tasks and $100+$ objects, organized into six physics-grounded interaction patterns. Building on this dataset, we propose \textbf{OmniVTA}, a world-model-based visuo-tactile manipulation framework that integrates four tightly coupled modules: a self-supervised tactile encoder, a two-stream visuo-tactile world model for predicting short-horizon contact evolution, a contact-aware fusion policy for action generation, and a 60Hz reflexive controller that corrects deviations between predicted and observed tactile signals in a closed loop. Real-robot experiments across all six interaction categories show that OmniVTA outperforms existing methods and generalizes well to unseen objects and geometric configurations, confirming the value of combining predictive contact modeling with high-frequency tactile feedback for contact-rich manipulation. All data, models, and code will be made publicly available on the project website at https://mrsecant.github.io/OmniVTA.

</details>


### [57] [NavTrust: Benchmarking Trustworthiness for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19229)
*Huaide Jiang,Yash Chaudhary,Yuping Wang,Zehao Wang,Raghav Sharma,Manan Mehta,Yang Zhou,Lichao Sun,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavTrust是首个统一评估具身导航系统在真实世界模态损坏下鲁棒性的基准，涵盖RGB、深度和指令的多种损坏类型，揭示了现有SOTA方法性能显著下降，并提出了有效的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航研究主要在理想条件下评估模型性能，忽视了真实世界中可能出现的各种模态损坏（如RGB图像噪声、深度传感器误差、指令歧义等），缺乏系统性评估这些损坏对导航性能影响的统一基准。

Method: 提出NavTrust基准，系统性地在真实场景中损坏输入模态（RGB、深度、指令），评估其对导航性能的影响。基准包含多种RGB-Depth损坏类型和指令变体，在统一框架下测试了7种SOTA方法，并评估了4种缓解策略以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 对7种SOTA方法的广泛评估显示，在真实损坏下导航性能显著下降，揭示了关键的鲁棒性差距。四种缓解策略有效提升了模型对RGB-Depth和指令损坏的鲁棒性。在真实移动机器人上的部署验证了改进后的模型对损坏具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NavTrust是首个统一评估具身导航系统模态损坏鲁棒性的基准，揭示了现有方法的脆弱性，为构建更可信赖的具身导航系统提供了路线图，并展示了缓解策略的有效性。

Abstract: There are two major categories of embodied navigation: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), where agents navigate by following natural language instructions; and Object-Goal Navigation (OGN), where agents navigate to a specified target object. However, existing work primarily evaluates model performance under nominal conditions, overlooking the potential corruptions that arise in real-world settings. To address this gap, we present NavTrust, a unified benchmark that systematically corrupts input modalities, including RGB, depth, and instructions, in realistic scenarios and evaluates their impact on navigation performance. To our best knowledge, NavTrust is the first benchmark that exposes embodied navigation agents to diverse RGB-Depth corruptions and instruction variations in a unified framework. Our extensive evaluation of seven state-of-the-art approaches reveals substantial performance degradation under realistic corruptions, which highlights critical robustness gaps and provides a roadmap toward more trustworthy embodied navigation systems. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate four distinct mitigation strategies to enhance robustness against RGB-Depth and instructions corruptions. Our base models include Uni-NaVid and ETPNav. We deployed them on a real mobile robot and observed improved robustness to corruptions. The project website is: https://navtrust.github.io.

</details>


### [58] [Not All Features Are Created Equal: A Mechanistic Study of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19233)
*Bryce Grant,Xijia Zhao,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过激活注入、稀疏自编码器和线性探针等方法，系统分析了6个VLA模型（80M-7B参数）在机器人控制任务中的工作机制，发现视觉通路主导动作生成，语言敏感性取决于任务结构而非模型设计，并揭示了专家通路与VLM通路的功能分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA模型将感知、语言和运动控制整合到单一架构中，但这些模型如何将多模态输入转化为动作的机制仍然不明确。研究者希望通过系统分析不同规模VLA模型的内在表示和工作原理，揭示其动作生成的机制。

Method: 采用激活注入、稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和线性探针三种方法，对6个参数规模从80M到7B的VLA模型进行分析。在4个基准测试上进行了超过394,000次rollout实验，包括跨任务激活注入、零提示注入、子空间注入等实验设计。

Result: 1) 视觉通路在所有架构中主导动作生成：基线激活注入能恢复近相同行为，跨任务注入使机器人趋向源任务位置（X-VLA中99.8%的episodes与源轨迹对齐）；2) 语言敏感性取决于任务结构而非模型设计：当视觉上下文唯一指定任务时语言被忽略，当多个目标共享场景时语言变得关键；3) 专家通路编码运动程序，VLM通路编码目标语义；4) 对比识别恢复了82+个操作概念，因果消融显示敏感性范围28-92%零效应率。

Conclusion: VLA模型的动作生成主要由视觉通路驱动，形成与场景坐标绑定的空间运动程序。语言的作用取决于任务结构而非模型架构。多通路架构中专家通路和VLM通路功能分离，分别编码运动程序和目标语义。研究发布了Action Atlas平台用于交互式探索VLA表示。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine perception, language, and motor control in a single architecture, yet how they translate multimodal inputs into actions remains poorly understood. We apply activation injection, sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and linear probes to six models spanning 80M--7B parameters across 394,000+ rollout episodes on four benchmarks. The visual pathway dominates action generation across all architectures: injecting baseline activations into null-prompt episodes recovers near-identical behavior, while cross-task injection steers robots toward source-task positions (99.8\% of X-VLA episodes align with the source trajectory), exposing spatially bound motor programs tied to scene coordinates rather than abstract task representations. Language sensitivity depends on task structure, not model design: when visual context uniquely specifies the task, language is ignored; when multiple goals share a scene, language becomes essential (X-VLA \texttt{libero\_goal}: 94\%$\to$10\% under wrong prompts vs.\ \texttt{libero\_object}: 60--100\% regardless). In all three multi-pathway architectures (\pizhalf{}, SmolVLA, GR00T), expert pathways encode motor programs while VLM pathways encode goal semantics ($2\times$ greater behavioral displacement from expert injection), and subspace injection confirms these occupy separable activation subspaces. Per-token SAE processing is essential for action fidelity on most architectures, though mean-pooling improves fidelity on X-VLA. Contrastive identification recovers 82+ manipulation concepts, and causal ablation reveals sensitivity spanning 28--92\% zero-effect rates independent of representation width. We release \textbf{Action Atlas} (https://action-atlas.com) for interactive exploration of VLA representations across all six models.

</details>

### [59] [Probabilistic multivariate statistical process control via kernel parameter uncertainty propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19055)
*Zina-Sabrina Duma,Victoria Jorry,Ayesha Safraz,Maria Paola di Crosta,Tuomas Sihvonen,Lassi Roininen,Satu-Pia Reinikainen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出概率核多元统计过程控制框架，量化并传播核参数不确定性到监控统计量中，通过贝叶斯推断和MCMC方法处理核参数不确定性，在田纳西伊士曼过程基准测试中验证了框架有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统K-MSPC中核参数通常通过启发式或确定性优化选择并固定处理，但实际中这些参数是从有限噪声数据中推断的，忽略参数不确定性会导致过度自信的控制限和不稳定的报警行为，特别是在核选择不确定时。

Method: 采用两阶段工作流程：1) 使用监督或无监督模型进行确定性核校准；2) 通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛进行核参数的贝叶斯推断。将后验样本通过核主成分分析传播，生成概率性的T²和平方预测误差控制图，以及考虑不确定性的贡献图。

Result: 在田纳西伊士曼过程基准测试中，对于平方指数核，后验均值监控通常比确定性先验均值图有更好的故障检测性能；控制状态下可信带保持较窄，故障状态下变宽，反映了异常状态下认知不确定性的放大；自动相关性确定核减少了后验不确定性，性能接近确定性基线；无监督校准产生更宽的后验带但仍有稳健的故障检测。

Conclusion: 提出的概率K-MSPC框架成功量化并传播了核参数不确定性，提供了更可靠的监控统计量和控制限，能够反映不同运行状态下的认知不确定性，为工业过程监控提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Kernel-based multivariate statistical process control (K-MSPC) extends classical monitoring to nonlinear industrial processes. Its performance depends critically on kernel parameters such as lengthscales and variance terms. In current practice these parameters are typically selected by heuristics or deterministic optimisation, and then treated as fixed, despite being inferred from finite and noisy data. This can lead to overconfident control limits and unstable alarm behaviour when the kernel choice is uncertain. This work proposes a probabilistic K-MSPC framework that quantifies and propagates kernel parameter uncertainty to the monitoring statistics. The approach follows a two-stage workflow: (i) deterministic kernel calibration using supervised or unsupervised models, and (ii) Bayesian inference of kernel parameters via Markov chain Monte Carlo. Posterior samples are propagated through kernel Principal Component Analysis to produce probabilistic $T^2$ and squarred prediction error control charts, together with uncertainty-aware contribution plots. The framework is evaluated on the Tennessee Eastman Process benchmark. Results show that posterior-mean monitoring often improves fault detection compared to deterministic prior-mean charts for the squared exponential kernel, while credible bands remain narrow in-control and widen under faults, reflecting amplified epistemic uncertainty in abnormal regimes. The automatic relevance determination kernel reduces posterior uncertainty and yields performance close to the deterministic baseline, whereas unsupervised calibration produces wider posterior bands but still robust fault detection.

</details>


### [60] [The Uncertain Policy Price of Scaling Direct Air Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19143)
*Leonardo Chiani,Pietro Andreoni,Laurent Drouet,Tobias Schmidt,Katrin Sievert,Bjerne Steffen,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文对直接空气碳捕集与封存（DACCS）技术进行了首次不确定性量化和全局敏感性分析，发现DACCS部署呈现厚尾分布，仅有4-6%概率在本世纪中叶实现十亿吨级碳移除，且需要持续数十年的高额补贴（200-330美元/吨CO₂）和强减排政策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DACCS作为一种有前景的CO₂移除技术，其大规模部署仍具推测性，但现有研究往往忽视了该技术在技术、经济、政策等方面的不确定性。本文旨在填补这一空白，对DACCS的不确定性进行系统量化分析。

Method: 使用详细过程的综合评估模型和新开发的敏感性算法，对DACCS的技术、市场、金融和公共支持驱动因素进行不确定性量化和全局敏感性分析。

Result: DACCS部署呈现厚尾分布：多数情景显示技术采用有限，但有4-6%的概率在本世纪中叶实现十亿吨级碳移除。要达到此规模需要持续数十年的高额补贴（200-330美元/吨CO₂），形成9000-30000亿美元的公共支持计划。这种投资只有在强减排政策配合下才能在本世纪中叶获得回报。

Conclusion: 研究强调了气候政策在实现稳健且经济可持续的CO₂移除战略中的关键作用。DACCS的大规模部署不仅需要巨额公共资金支持，还必须与强减排政策相结合才能实现经济可持续性。

Abstract: Direct air carbon capture and storage (DACCS) is a promising CO2 removal technology, but its deployment at scale remains speculative. Yet, its technological, economic, and policy-related uncertainties have often been overlooked in mitigation pathways. This paper conducts the first uncertainty quantification and global sensitivity analysis of DACCS on technological, market, financial and public support drivers, using a detailed-process Integrated Assessment Model and newly developed sensitivity algorithms. We find that DACCS deployment exhibits a fat-tailed distribution: most scenarios show modest technology uptake, but there is a small but non-zero probability (4-6%) of achieving gigaton-scale removals by mid-century. Scaling DACCS to gigaton levels requires subsidies that always exceed 200-330 USD/tCO2 and are sustained for decades, resulting in a public support programme of 900-3000 USD Billions. Such an effort pays back by mid-century, but only if accompanied by strong emission reduction policies. These findings highlight the critical role of climate policies in enabling a robust and economically sustainable CO2 removal strategy.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [61] [On the equivalence of semi-discrete Active Flux and Discontinuous Galerkin methods and a comparison of their performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18160)
*Wasilij Barsukow,Christian Klingenberg,Simon Krotsch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: Active Flux方法比DG方法更经济，但两者在特定条件下是等价的，Radau多项式揭示了DG的超收敛性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在揭示Active Flux（AF）方法和Discontinuous Galerkin（DG）方法之间的深层联系。虽然AF方法使用全局连续近似，在相同自由度下能达到更高阶多项式近似，但作者发现这两种看似不同的方法在某些条件下实际上是等价的。

Method: 通过建立AF和DG方法自由度之间的映射关系，证明在特定条件下两种方法的更新公式是一致的。对于线性问题（一维和多维）以及某种意义上的非线性问题，半离散的AF和DG方法是相同的。特别地，作者展示了Radau多项式及其零点在映射中的关键作用。

Result: 1. 建立了AF和DG自由度之间的映射关系，证明两者更新公式一致；2. 在数值实验中确认AF方法比DG方法更经济（相同误差下）；3. 揭示了Radau多项式及其零点在映射中的关键作用，解释了DG方法的超收敛性；4. 在Radau点处，AF作为高阶背景方案"显现"出来。

Conclusion: AF方法在相同自由度下能达到更高阶近似，比DG更经济。然而，两者在特定条件下是等价的，这种等价性为理解DG方法的超收敛性提供了自然框架。Radau多项式及其零点在映射中的出现表明，在Radau点处AF作为高阶背景方案"显现"出来，这为理解DG方法的数值特性提供了新视角。

Abstract: The Active Flux (AF) method employs a globally continuous approximation, like continuous Finite Element methods. This is achieved through the placement of point values at cell interfaces which are shared between adjacent cells. With, on average, K+1 degrees of freedom per cell, Active Flux achieves a polynomial approximation of degree K+1, while the Discontinuous Galerkin (DG) method uses only polynomials of degree K, i.e. one degree less with the same number of degrees of freedom. Despite all the differences, in this paper we show, however, that for linear problems in one and several dimensions as well as -- in some sense -- for nonlinear ones, semi-discrete AF and DG are the same method. We identify a mapping between their respective degrees of freedom, upon which the updates of these degrees of freedom turn out to agree. On the one hand, AF therefore seems more economical then DG for a given value of the error, and we confirm this in numerical experiments. On the other hand, this is a way to understand superconvergence of DG in a natural way, and we show how Radau polynomials and their zeros appear in the mapping between DG and AF: In the Radau points, AF "shines through" as the background high-order scheme behind DG.

</details>


### [62] [Splitting-strategies for arbitrary-order fully mixed finite element discretizations of the Biot equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18241)
*Fleurianne Bertrand,Jakub Wiktor Both,Tugay Dağlı*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Biot方程的全混合公式，通过弱对称性约束实现流固耦合对称结构，建立了稳定的混合有限元方法，并分析了迭代分裂策略的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Biot方程的全混合公式，利用其对称耦合结构，避免子物理问题间的强兼容性条件，同时保持两个子问题的守恒结构。

Method: 采用全混合有限元方法，弱对称性约束弹性应力张量，建立鞍点结构，分析inf-sup稳定性，研究迭代分裂策略（固定应力分裂加调优）。

Result: 建立了任意阶离散空间的inf-sup稳定性，获得最优先验误差估计，证明了迭代分裂策略的收敛性和收敛速率，理论验证了负稳定化的有效性。

Conclusion: 全混合公式为Biot方程提供了灵活稳定的数值框架，弱对称性约束和迭代分裂策略有效处理流固耦合问题，数值结果支持理论分析。

Abstract: We study the fully mixed formulation of the Biot equations, which is characterized by a symmetric coupling between flow and deformation. This structure enables the use of stable mixed finite elements for each subproblem without a strong compatibility condition across the two subphysics. To exploit this flexibility while preserving the conservation structure of both subproblems, we consider fully mixed finite element methods in which the symmetry of the elastic stress tensor is enforced weakly. The resulting mixed formulation exhibits a saddle-point structure whose stability is determined by suitable inf--sup conditions. Inf--sup stability is established for several families of discrete spaces of arbitrary order, leading to optimal a priori error estimates. Iterative splitting strategies following the classical fixed-stress split with additional tuning are specifically investigated for the fully mixed formulation, with proof of convergence and rates depending on the coupling strength. Contrary to previous analyses on coupled problems with a symmetric structure, we theoretically prove the efficacy of negative stabilization, consistent with Schur-complement ideas. Numerical results based on analytical solutions and the classical Mandel problem support the theory.

</details>


### [63] [Christoffel Adaptive Sampling for Sparse Random Feature Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18251)
*Ben Adcock,Khiem Can,Xuemeng Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文将主动学习与稀疏随机特征展开相结合，通过Christoffel函数指导自适应采样过程，在数据稀缺环境下提高采样效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏随机特征展开在数据稀缺环境下具有优势，但传统采样策略效率有限。需要开发自适应采样方法来优化样本点分布，减少数据采集成本

Method: 将主动学习与稀疏随机特征近似结合，利用Christoffel函数指导自适应采样过程。通过稀疏恢复求解器获得的迭代选择基函数，动态选择对函数空间贡献最大的样本点

Result: 数值实验比较了自适应和非自适应采样策略在SRFE框架下的表现。结果显示自适应采样在保持高精度的同时显著降低了样本复杂度

Abstract: Random Feature Models (RFMs) have become a powerful tool for approximating multivariate functions and solving partial differential equations efficiently. Sparse Random Feature Expansions (SRFE) improve traditional RFMs by incorporating sparsity, making it particularly effective in data-scarce settings. In this work, we integrate active learning with sparse random feature approximations to improve sampling efficiency. Specifically, we incorporate the Christoffel function to guide an adaptive sampling process, dynamically selecting informative sample points based on their contribution to the function space. This approach optimizes the distribution of sample points by leveraging the Christoffel function associated with an iteratively-chosen basis obtained by the sparse recovery solver. We conduct numerical experiments comparing adaptive and nonadaptive sampling strategies with the SRFE framework and examine their accuracy for various function approximation tasks. Overall, our results demonstrate the advantages of adaptive sampling in maintaining high accuracy while reducing sample complexity for SRFE, highlighting its potential for scientific computing tasks where data is expensive to acquire.

Method: 在连续求和分部（C-SBP）框架内，针对具有齐次通量函数的标量和对称双曲系统，建立稳定性-一致性论证，推导出非线性误差演化不等式。该不等式的解提供了数值误差的显式上界。证明当网格间距足够小，且对于d维空间中p>1+d/2的p阶C-SBP离散化，该误差界在任何有限时间区间内保持有限，并随网格细化趋于零。

Result: 证明了在光滑解和通量假设下，熵稳定的分裂形式离散化对于标量和对称双曲系统具有收敛性。无粘性Burgers方程作为典型示例。结果显示，即使存在局部线性不稳定性，只要满足p>1+d/2的条件，数值误差在有限时间内有界且随网格细化趋于零。

Abstract: The Lax equivalence theorem guarantees convergence of stable and consistent discretizations for linear hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs). For nonlinear problems, however, stability and consistency alone do not generally guarantee convergence, even for smooth solutions, and existing convergence results typically rely either on projection-based error decompositions or on linearization arguments that do not directly extend to entropy-stable split-form discretizations. In particular, general convergence results for entropy-stable discretizations of hyperbolic PDEs are currently lacking, despite their widespread use. In this work, we prove convergence under smoothness assumptions on the exact solution and fluxes for entropy-stable split-form discretizations of scalar and symmetric hyperbolic systems with homogeneous flux functions within the continuous summation-by-parts (C-SBP) framework. The scalar inviscid Burgers equation is presented as a canonical example. The analysis is based on a stability-consistency argument that yields a nonlinear error evolution inequality whose solution provides an explicit upper bound on the numerical error. We show that, for sufficiently small mesh spacing, and for degree-$p$ C-SBP discretizations in $d$ spatial dimensions with $p>1+d/2$, this bound remains finite on any finite time interval and tends to zero as the mesh is refined, implying convergence despite the presence of local linear instabilities. The results help clarify the relationship between consistency, entropy stability, nonlinear error growth, and convergence for discretizations of nonlinear hyperbolic problems.

Result: 该方法在任意光滑多维域（包括凹几何区域附近）保持小波阶数，能够在复杂域中进行网格自适应，并通过手动设置的细化阈值稳健地限制数值误差。在静态和动态问题（包括具有移动边界和时间自适应网格分辨率的Navier-Stokes方程）上验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了有效的网格自适应，在用户定义的细化阈值与整体解误差之间建立了稳健、可预测的关系，即使对于具有复杂移动边界的问题也是如此。

Abstract: Wavelet-based grid adaptation methods use multiresolution analysis for error estimation, offering a mathematically rigorous approach to adaptive grid refinement when solving Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). However, applying these methods to PDE discretizations with immersed geometries is challenging, as standard interpolating wavelet transforms lose consistency near non-grid-aligned boundary intersections. To address this, we propose a high-order interpolating wavelet transform adaptation strategy compatible with sharp immersed boundary and interface discretizations. The approach performs consistent high-order wavelet transforms on narrow intervals using a 1D polynomial extrapolation technique. To maintain high order, the technique incorporates boundary values and derivatives, which are evaluated from multivariate interpolating polynomials similar to those used in high order immersed finite difference discretizations. Consequently, the proposed approach maintains the wavelet order on any arbitrary smooth multidimensional domain, including near concave geometry sections. This approach enables grid adaptation in complex domains while robustly bounding the numerical error via a manually set refinement threshold. The algorithm's performance is validated on both static and dynamic problems, including the Navier-Stokes equations with moving boundaries and temporally adapting grid resolutions. The results demonstrate that the proposed method enables effective grid adaptation, establishing a robust, predictable relationship between a user-defined refinement threshold and the overall solution error, even for problems with complex, moving boundaries.

</details>


### [66] [Born-Series-Inspired Residual Metric for Learning-based Preconditioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18527)
*Juntao Wang,Xinliang Liu,Jiwei Jia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于神经算子的预条件Born级数方法(NPBS)，通过引入与参考格林算子相关的Riesz映射残差度量来优化学习型PDE预条件器，特别适用于高频Helmholtz方程等病态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习型PDE预条件器通常优化未预条件的欧几里得残差范数，但对于高频Helmholtz方程等不定算子，这种默认度量会使学习和迭代校正对近共振谱分量过于敏感。经典预条件方法通过重塑残差几何来成功解决这一问题，因此需要开发与预条件策略相匹配的残差度量。

Method: 基于Born级数和移位拉普拉斯左预条件之间的联系，推导出参考格林算子G_η作为自然Riesz映射残差度量R_η=G_η*G_η。提出神经预条件Born级数(NPBS)迭代，用残差驱动的神经算子替换标量CBS松弛，并采用度量匹配的Born级数启发损失函数L_bs^{R_η}。该方法架构无关，支持通过FFT/DST/DCT实现O(NlogN)快速评估。

Result: 在异构Helmholtz问题上的数值实验证明了该方法的有效性，且随着系统病态程度增加，其优势更加明显。该方法还扩展到其他PDE类别，包括对流-扩散-反应方程和非线性PDE的线性化牛顿系统，在这些应用中也能显著减少迭代次数。

Conclusion: 通过将经典预条件策略的几何洞察与神经算子相结合，提出的NPBS框架为学习型PDE求解器提供了更合适的残差度量，特别适用于病态系统，并能扩展到多种PDE类型，实现了迭代效率的显著提升。

Abstract: Loss functions for learning-based PDE preconditioners implicitly choose a \emph{metric} in which residuals are matched, yet most approaches still optimize an unpreconditioned Euclidean residual norm. For indefinite operators such as the high-frequency Helmholtz equation, this default metric can make both learning and iterative correction overly sensitive to near-resonant spectral components, while classical preconditioning succeeds precisely by reshaping the residual geometry. We show that the Born Series and shifted-Laplacian left preconditioning are linked by the identity $
I-G_ηV_η= G_ηA = L_η^{-1}A, $
which turns the reference Green operator $G_η$ into a natural Riesz-map residual metric $
R_η= G_η^\ast G_η$
and suggests measuring the physical residual via $
\|r\|_{R_η}=\|G_ηr\|_2. $
Building on this viewpoint, we propose a \emph{Neural Preconditioned Born Series} (NPBS) iteration that replaces the scalar CBS relaxation with a residual-driven neural operator, together with a metric-matched Born-series-inspired loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{bs}}^{R_η}$. The framework is architecture-agnostic and supports fast $\mathcal{O}(N\log N)$ evaluation via FFT/DST/DCT. Numerical experiments on heterogeneous Helmholtz problems demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, and its advantage becomes more pronounced as the systems grow more ill-conditioned; we then extend the framework to other PDE classes, including convection--diffusion--reaction equations and linearized Newton systems for nonlinear PDEs, where it also yields substantial iteration reductions.

</details>


### [67] [A stable and fast method for solving multibody scattering problems via the method of fundamental solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19113)
*Yunhui Cai,Joar Bagge,Per-Gunnar Martinsson*

TL;DR: 提出一种用于求解二维和三维声学多体散射问题的数值方法，通过局部计算每个物体的散射算子，然后组合成全局线性系统，即使使用病态方法（如基本解方法）也能保持全局求解器的可扩展性和数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多体散射问题时，传统边界积分方程方法（如Nyström或Galerkin方法）实现复杂，而基本解方法（MFS）虽然简单但通常病态。需要一种既能利用MFS简单性，又能保持全局求解器稳定性和可扩展性的方法。

Method: 通过局部计算为每个散射体构建高精度散射算子（散射矩阵），然后将这些局部散射矩阵组合形成全局线性系统。即使局部计算使用病态的基本解方法（MFS），全局系统仍能保持良好条件数。系统适合迭代求解器，并可通过快速多极子方法等加速矩阵向量乘法。

Result: 该方法产生的系数矩阵相对良态，即使处理大量散射体时也是如此。全局线性系统适合迭代求解器，并能通过快速算法加速。关键创新在于允许使用病态的MFS构建局部散射矩阵，同时保持全局求解器的可扩展性和数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种简单有效的数值方案，利用基本解方法的实现简单性，同时通过局部-全局框架克服了其病态性，为大规模多体散射问题提供了可扩展且数值稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: The paper describes a numerical method for solving acoustic multibody scattering problems in two and three dimensions. The idea is to compute a highly accurate approximation to the scattering operator for each body through a local computation, and then use these scattering matrices to form a global linear system. The resulting coefficient matrix is relatively well-conditioned, even for problems involving a very large number of scatterers. The linear system is amenable to iterative solvers, and can readily be accelerated via fast algorithms for the matrix-vector multiplication such as the fast multipole method. The key point of the work is that the local scattering matrices can be constructed using potentially ill-conditioned techniques such as the method of fundamental solutions (MFS), while still maintaining scalability and numerical stability of the global solver. The resulting algorithm is simple, as the MFS is far simpler to implement than alternative techniques based on discretizing boundary integral equations using Nyström or Galerkin.

</details>


### [68] [Reconstructions of Single Pixel X-Ray Transforms with Applications in Nuclear-Disarmament Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18728)
*Christopher Fichtlscherer,R. Scott Kemp,Christina Brandt*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于单像素X射线变换的核武器验证方法，分析了该变换的数学特性，并开发了基于Douglas-Rachford分裂和全变分去噪的有限测量值重建算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在核军控和裁军过程中，需要在不泄露敏感信息的情况下验证物体是否为核武器。现有的单像素X射线变换方法在数学分析上有限，需要深入研究其数学特性并开发有效的重建算法。

Method: 1. 分析单像素X射线变换的数学特性：非线性、连续性、Fréchet可微性和凸性；2. 提出基于Douglas-Rachford分裂和全变分去噪的重建方法，特别针对旋转对称物体使用一维直接全变分去噪算法。

Result: 证明了单像素X射线变换具有非线性、连续、Fréchet可微和凸的数学特性；开发了能够从有限个含噪声的单像素测量值重建物体的算法实现。

Conclusion: 单像素X射线变换具有良好数学特性，可用于核验证应用；提出的重建算法能够有效处理有限测量数据，特别适用于旋转对称物体的重建，为核武器验证提供了数学基础。

Abstract: In nuclear arms control and disarmament processes, it is crucial to determine whether an object is a nuclear weapon or not without revealing sensitive information about it. At the MIT: Laboratory for Nuclear Security and Policy, such a nuclear verification method was developed, showcasing a transmission-based approach [1]. This method's essential part rests on a mathematical operation, the Single-Pixel X-Ray Transform: a cone of X-rays transmits an object and the remaining intensity is measured with a single-pixel detector. This transformation and the recovery of objects from dimensionless single-pixel measurements more generally has only been analyzed to a limited extent. In this work, we investigate some of the Single Pixel X-Ray Transform's mathematical properties. More specifically, we show that the Single Pixel X-ray transform is non-linear, continuous, Fréchet-differentiable and convex. We also introduce a method of reconstructing an object based only on a finite number of dimensionless, noisy Single Pixel X-Ray Transform measurement values. This method is based on Douglas-Rachford splitting and uses total variation denoising. We present an implementation for this method, focusing on rotational symmetric objects, as they allow the use of a one-dimensional direct total variation denoising algorithm [2].

</details>

*Xiuzhu Yang,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: IPA-AC方法在固定非局部模型下具有二阶收敛性，但对非局部长度尺度敏感，不满足渐近兼容条件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管IPA-AC方法在经验上成功抑制了边界引起的几何误差，但其在不同尺度极限下的收敛行为缺乏系统的理论表征

Method: 通过Lax等价定理建立统一收敛框架，推导误差估计，分析三个关键极限区域：固定视域δ下的网格细化、固定网格下的视域变化、以及渐近兼容性

Abstract: The Improved Partial Area-Analytical Calculation (IPA-AC) method represents a leading meshfree discretization strategy for peridynamic models, distinguished by its rigorous geometric treatment of boundary intersections via dual corrections of integration weights and quadrature points. Despite its empirical success in suppressing boundary-induced geometric errors, a systematic theoretical characterization of its convergence behaviors under distinct scaling limits has remained elusive. This work establishes a unified convergence framework for the IPA-AC method applied to both scalar and tensor kernels. By leveraging the Lax Equivalence Theorem, we explicitly derive error estimates that reveal the method's performance across three critical limiting regimes. The theoretical analysis, substantiated by numerical validation, demonstrates that: (1) for a fixed horizon $δ$, the method achieves robust second-order convergence $\mathcal{O}(h ^{2})$ with respect to the mesh size $h$; (2) for a fixed mesh, the discretization error scales as $\mathcal{O}(δ^{-2})$, indicating a sensitivity to the nonlocal length scale; and (3) the method does not satisfy the Asymptotic Compatibility (AC) condition. These findings clarify that while the IPA-AC method offers superior accuracy for simulating fixed nonlocal models, it requires a sufficiently large horizon-to-mesh ratio to mitigate intrinsic discretization errors when approximating the local limit.

</details>


### [70] [Model Order Reduction of Cerebrovascular Hemodynamics Using POD_Galerkin and Reservoir Computing_based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18837)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高效准确的降阶模型来模拟脑血管系统的非定常血流动力学，以替代计算成本高昂的完整3D计算流体动力学模拟，为临床血流动力学分析提供快速计算工具。

Method: 首先使用本征正交分解将理想化基底动脉分叉的高保真3D CFD快照压缩到低维潜在空间。对比两种方法：1) POD-Galerkin模型：将Navier-Stokes方程投影到降阶基上；2) POD-Reservoir Computing模型：通过循环架构学习系数的时间演化。引入多谐波多振幅训练信号提高训练效率。

Result: 两种方法均实现了相对于完整阶模拟10^2到10^3倍的计算加速，能够准确预测壁面剪切应力等流动量，展示了作为高效准确替代模型的潜力。

Conclusion: 基于物理的侵入式POD-Galerkin和数据驱动的非侵入式POD-Reservoir Computing都是有效的脑血管血流动力学降阶模拟策略，具有显著的计算效率优势，为快速血流动力学评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We investigate model order reduction (MOR) strategies for simulating unsteady hemodynamics within cerebrovascular systems, contrasting a physics-based intrusive approach with a data-driven non-intrusive framework. High-fidelity 3D Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) snapshots of an idealised basilar artery bifurcation are first compressed into a low-dimensional latent space using Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD). We evaluate the performance of a POD-Galerkin (POD-G) model, which projects the Navier-Stokes equations onto the reduced basis, against a POD-Reservoir Computing (POD-RC) model that learns the temporal evolution of coefficients through a recurrent architecture. A multi-harmonic and multi-amplitude training signal is introduced to improve training efficiency. Both methodologies achieve computational speed-ups on the order of 10^2 to 10^3 compared to full-order simulations, demonstrating their potential as efficient and accurate surrogates for predicting flow quantities such as wall shear stress.

</details>

Abstract: We derive an identity that relates a class of multiple integrals involving Vandermonde polynomials to divided differences. Alternatively the identity can be viewed as an integral formula for divided differences. As part of the derivation we show that both sums of pure partial derivatives and mixed partial derivatives of Vandermonde polynomials are zero, which might be of independent interest.

### [72] [A Stabilized Mortar Method for Discontinuities in Geological Media with Non-Conforming Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18905)
*Daniele Moretto,Andrea Franceschini,Massimiliano Ferronato*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 断层和裂缝力学的精确数值模拟对地下系统性能和安全评估至关重要，但间断表面的离散化表示和摩擦接触行为的鲁棒模拟仍面临重大挑战。

Method: 使用mortar方法施加接触约束，允许断层界面周围的非一致性网格。采用分段线性位移和断层一侧（非mortar侧）分段常数拉格朗日乘子表示界面牵引力。通过开发牵引力跳跃稳定项来稳定违反inf-sup条件的公式，使用宏单元分析自动评估稳定化缩放，无需用户选择额外参数。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提公式不仅恢复了inf-sup稳定性条件，而且在更细的非mortar侧情况下也能恢复稳定的牵引力分布，而其他inf-sup稳定公式在此情况下会失效。该方法成功模拟了工业地质应用中典型非一致性角点网格的非线性接触条件。

Conclusion: 提出的稳定mortar方法为断层力学模拟提供了一种鲁棒的数值框架，能够处理非一致性网格并自动处理稳定性问题，适用于工业地质应用中的复杂接触条件模拟。

Abstract: Accurate numerical simulation of fault and fracture mechanics is critical for the performance and safety assessment of many subsurface systems. The discretized representation of discontinuity surfaces and the robust simulation of their frictional contact behavior still represent major challenges. In this work, we use the mortar method to enforce the contact constraints and allow for non-conformity around the discontinuity surface, with a set of Lagrange multipliers playing the role of interface tractions. The formulation combines piecewise linear displacements with piecewise constant multipliers defined on one side of the fault interface (the non-mortar side). This choice for the Lagrange multipliers has a number of advantages from practical and computational viewpoints, but violates the inf-sup stability constraint. In order to stabilize the proposed formulation, we develop a traction-jump stabilization term to be added to the constraint equations. We use a macro-element analysis to derive an algorithmic strategy that automatically evaluates the proper scaling of the stabilization, without requiring any additional user-selected parameter. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed formulation not only restores the inf-sup stability condition, but also recovers stable traction profiles in the presence of finer non-mortar sides, where other inf-sup-stable formulations fail. The proposed method is finally used to simulate non-linear contact conditions in non-conforming corner-point grids typically used in industrial geological applications.

</details>


### [73] [Non-asymptotic uniform in time error bounds for new and old numerical schemes for SPDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18944)
*Can Huang,Michela Ottobre,Gideon Simpson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了随机偏微分方程(SPDEs)的数值格式，提出了证明数值积分器非渐近、时间一致误差界的通用方法，特别关注了非全局Lipschitz非线性项(如随机Allen-Cahn方程)的情况，分析了三种避免有限时间爆炸的数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SPDEs数值格式的动机在于：经典半隐式欧拉时间离散化在处理非全局Lipschitz非线性项(如随机Allen-Cahn方程)时可能出现有限时间爆炸问题，需要开发能够同时准确捕捉瞬态和长期动力学行为的稳定数值方法。

Method: 论文首先提出了证明数值积分器非渐近、时间一致误差界的通用判据。然后针对非全局Lipschitz非线性SPDEs，分析了三种数值格式：完全隐式格式和两种驯化(tamed)格式。这些格式考虑了完整的时空离散化，而不仅仅是时间离散。

Result: 证明了经典半隐式欧拉格式在Allen-Cahn类型SPDEs中可能出现有限时间爆炸。对于三种替代方案(完全隐式格式和两种驯化格式)，利用通用判据证明了非渐近、时间一致的误差界，并通过数值比较验证了这些格式的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文为SPDEs数值分析提供了统一的误差分析框架，特别解决了非全局Lipschitz非线性项带来的数值稳定性问题。提出的三种数值格式能够避免有限时间爆炸，同时准确捕捉方程的瞬态和长期行为，为实际计算提供了可靠的工具。

Abstract: We study numerical schemes for Stochastic Partial Differential Equations (SPDEs). We introduce a general method of proof of non-asymptotic uniform in time error bounds on numerical integrators for SPDEs, ensuring the schemes capture both the transient and the long term dynamics faithfully. We then consider SPDEs with non-globally Lipshitz nonlinearities, which include for example the stochastic Allen-Cahn equation and some stochastic advection-diffusion equations. For the case of Allen-Cahn type SPDEs we show that the classic semi-implicit Euler time-discretization can exhibit finite time blow up. This motivates analysing other schemes which do not suffer from this blow-up problem. We consider three numerical schemes for SPDEs with non globally Lipshitz nonlinearity: a fully implicit scheme and two tamed schemes. For these schemes we prove non-asymptotic uniform in time error bounds by leveraging our general criterion, and provide numerical comparisons. While the main emphasis in this paper is on the properties of the time-discretization, the schemes we consider are full space-time discretization of the SPDE.

</details>


### [74] [On Affordable High-Order Entropy-Conservative/Stable and Well-Balanced Methods for Nonconservative Hyperbolic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18978)
*Marco Artiano,Hendrik Ranocha*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了非保守双曲系统的熵保持波动形式，实现了算法化构建熵保持方法，并扩展到多维SBP框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有熵保持方法主要针对守恒系统，非保守系统的熵保持方法需要更简单、可解释且算法化的构建方式

Method: 提出特定形式的熵保持波动，分析充分必要条件，在有限体积框架内完全表征三点熵保持方法，扩展到多维SBP方法

Result: 获得了熵保持三点方法的完整特征，构建了多个新熵保持方案，数值实验验证了方案的鲁棒性和精度

Conclusion: 提出的波动形式为构建非保守双曲系统的熵保持方法提供了简单、算法化的途径，成功扩展到多维SBP框架

Abstract: Many entropy-conservative and entropy-stable (summarized as entropy-preserving) methods for hyperbolic conservation laws rely on Tadmor's theory for two-point entropy-preserving numerical fluxes and its higher-order extension via flux differencing using summation-by-parts (SBP) operators, e.g., in discontinuous Galerkin spectral element methods (DGSEMs). The underlying two-point formulations have been extended to nonconservative systems using fluctuations by Castro et al. (2013, doi:10.1137/110845379) with follow-up generalizations to SBP methods. We propose specific forms of entropy-preserving fluctuations for nonconservative hyperbolic systems that are simple to interpret and allow an algorithmic construction of entropy-preserving methods. We analyze necessary and sufficient conditions, and obtain a full characterization of entropy-preserving three-point methods within the finite volume framework. This formulation is extended to SBP methods in multiple space dimensions on Cartesian and curvilinear meshes. Additional properties such as well-balancedness extend naturally from the underlying finite volume method to the SBP framework. We use the algorithmic construction enabled by the chosen formulation to derive several new entropy-preserving schemes for nonconservative hyperbolic systems, e.g., the compressible Euler equations of an ideal gas using the internal energy equation and a dispersive shallow-water model. Numerical experiments show the robustness and accuracy of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [75] [A bilinear inverse problem with forward operator inaccuracy applied to neonatal atlas-based diffuse optical tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18980)
*Aada Hakula,Pauliina Hirvi,Nuutti Hyvönen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种处理线性逆问题中前向算子不准确性的方法，通过主成分分析建模候选前向算子的变化，将其转化为双线性张量逆问题，并开发了优化算法和吉布斯采样求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中线性逆问题的前向算子常基于系统近似构建，处理前向算子的不准确性是相对未充分研究的问题。特别是在医学成像等领域，使用图谱模型代替个体解剖模型会引入不准确性。

Method: 假设有一组候选前向算子矩阵，使用主成分分析(PCA)建模它们相对于均值的变异。将原始线性问题与包含的前向算子不准确性结合为双线性张量逆问题，提出了两种优化算法和吉布斯采样进行近似求解。

Result: 在弥散光学断层扫描的实际测试中，应用于新生儿(孕周29-44周)脑部解剖图谱模型代替个体模型时，重建的模拟血流动力学活动在空间定位和对比度噪声比方面获得了视觉和数值上的改进。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效处理前向算子的不准确性，在医学成像等实际应用中提高了重建质量，特别是在使用近似解剖模型时能获得更好的空间定位和对比度噪声比。

Abstract: Linear inverse problems are highly common in practical real-world applications from industry to medical imaging. The forward operator is often built on some approximations of the studied system. Handling inaccuracies in the forward operator in the context of inverse problems is a relatively unstudied problem. In this work, we assume that we have a set of candidate forward operator matrices and suggest principal component analysis (PCA) for modeling their variation from the mean. We combine the original linear problem with the included forward operator inaccuracy into a bilinear tensor inverse problem and present two optimization algorithms and Gibbs sampling for approximately solving the problem. As a real-world test case, we apply the algorithms to account for the inaccuracy that is present in the sensitivity profiles or Jacobian matrices in diffuse optical tomography when an atlas-based model of the head anatomy is used instead of the subject's own anatomical model in neonates over a wide range of gestational ages (29--44 weeks). We report visual and numerical improvements in the spatial localization and contrast-to-noise-ratio in reconstructions of simulated hemodynamic activity.

</details>

*Johnny Corbino*

Main category: math.NA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FDTD方法在电磁计算中应用广泛，但存在物理一致性方面的局限性。本文旨在开发一种基于拟态算子的有限差分方法，以更好地保持麦克斯韦方程的物理特性。

Method: 引入满足离散扩展高斯散度定理的拟态算子，构建紧凑且物理一致的电磁计算框架。使用MOLE库实现，并在一维正弦波与有耗介质相互作用、二维高斯脉冲与UPML吸收边界条件两个算例中验证。

Result: 成功开发了基于拟态算子的有限差分方法，能够有效求解一维和二维麦克斯韦方程。数值算例验证了该方法在处理介质相互作用和吸收边界条件方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 拟态有限差分方法为计算电磁学提供了一种物理一致且紧凑的数值框架，通过MOLE库实现验证了其在一维和二维问题中的适用性。

Abstract: We present a mimetic finite-difference approach for solving Maxwell's equations in one and two spatial dimensions. After introducing the governing equations and the classical Finite-Difference Time-Domain (FDTD) method, we describe mimetic operators that satisfy a discrete analogue of the extended Gauss divergence theorem and show how they lead to a compact, physically consistent formulation for computational electromagnetics. Two numerical examples are presented: a one-dimensional sinusoidal wave interacting with a lossy dielectric slab, and a two-dimensional Gaussian pulse with Uniaxial Perfectly Matched Layer (UPML) absorbing boundary conditions. All implementations use the Mimetic Operators Library Enhanced (MOLE).

</details>

*Timothy C. Andrews,Thomas M. Bendall*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在兼容有限元模型中，需要一种能够确保示踪剂质量守恒的输运方案，特别是在Charney-Phillips垂直交错网格中处理混合比的不同配置时，需要保持质量守恒和一致性。

Method: 采用保守输运方程求解示踪剂密度（混合比乘以干密度），而非使用混合比的平流输运方程。使用间断Galerkin输运方案，设计了适用于两种混合比配置（与干密度共位或垂直交错）的方法，并开发了质量守恒限制器以确保非负性。

Result: 新方案能够确保示踪剂密度守恒并保持一致性（维持恒定混合比）。通过终结者玩具化学测试和湿上升气泡测试验证了该方案在物理项中的使用，以及其在动力核心设置中准确模拟水分物种质量守恒的能力。

Conclusion: 该保守输运方案成功实现了标量示踪剂在兼容有限元模型中的质量守恒，适用于不同的网格配置，并通过限制器确保了非负性，为地球物理流体模拟提供了可靠的示踪剂输运方法。

Abstract: This paper outlines a conservative transport scheme for scalar tracers within a compatible finite element model for geophysical fluid equations. Instead of using the advective transport equation for a mixing ratio, a conservative transport equation is solved for the tracer density of the mixing ratio multiplied by the dry density. This ensures mass conservation in the continuous equations, which can be preserved in the discrete equations with a discontinuous Galerkin transport scheme. Our method is designed to work for two placements of the mixing ratio in a Charney-Phillips vertical staggering: either co-located with the dry density or vertically staggered from it. The new scheme is designed to conserve the tracer density and ensure consistency by maintaining a constant mixing ratio. Additionally, a mass-conserving limiter is developed to ensure non-negativity in the co-located configuration. Tests with terminator toy chemistry and a moist rising bubble show the use of the new transport scheme with physics terms and its ability to accurately model mass conservation of moisture species in a dynamical core setup.

</details>


### [78] [Reduced order computation of 2D elastodynamic Green's functions in layered soil using a low-rank tensor approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19080)
*Zainab Farooq,Amar Pashov,Pieter Reumers,Stijn François,Geert Degrande*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 基于Greedy Tucker Approximation的降阶建模策略，用于高效计算弹性动力学格林函数，显著降低计算和内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性动力学格林函数在多个源-接收位置、频率和材料属性下的评估计算成本高、内存需求大，特别是在参数化研究或边界元计算中。

Method: 采用Greedy Tucker Approximation策略，通过PGD型交替最小二乘法增量构建格林张量的低秩表示，使用Petrov-Galerkin公式提高收敛性和近似精度。

Result: 该方法将多维张量表示为一系列一维基函数和紧凑核心，显著减少了内存需求，在土壤层和层状半空间案例中得到验证，与直接刚度法结果一致。

Conclusion: 提出的降阶建模方法能有效处理弹性动力学格林函数的多维参数化计算，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算和内存成本。

Abstract: The evaluation of elastodynamic Green's functions across numerous source-receiver locations, frequencies, and material properties, particularly in the context of parametric studies or boundary element computations, is computationally demanding and memory intensive. This paper presents a reduced order modeling strategy based on the Greedy Tucker Approximation (GTA), which incrementally constructs a low-rank representation of the Green's tensor through rank-one enrichments obtained via a Proper Generalized Decomposition (PGD)-type alternating least squares procedure. A Petrov-Galerkin formulation is employed to improve convergence and approximation accuracy. The resulting multi-dimensional tensor, expressed in terms of one-dimensional basis functions and a compact core, achieves substantial reductions in memory requirements. The methodology is demonstrated for two cases: a soil layer on rigid bedrock and a layered halfspace. Different separable dimensions are considered to capture various combinations of source and receiver configurations, frequencies, and material parameters. Results are validated against those obtained with the direct stiffness method and computation times and memory requirements are compared.

</details>


### [79] [Numerical Considerations for the Construction of Karhunen-Loève Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19108)
*Cosmin Safta,Habib N. Najm*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该报告研究了构建二阶随机过程Karhunen-Loève展开（KLE）的数值方法，分析了Fredholm积分方程离散化与样本矩阵奇异值分解的代数等价性，并通过解析解基准评估数值精度，探讨了高维不规则域和拓扑复杂域中的KLE构建问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究KLE数值构造中的关键问题：如何确保基于Fredholm积分方程离散化的特征分解与基于数据样本的奇异值分解方法在代数上等价且数值上一致，特别是在复杂几何域和高维设置中。

Method: 1. 推导Fredholm积分方程离散化特征分解与加权样本矩阵奇异值分解的代数等价性；2. 使用指数和平方指数协方差核的解析特征解作为基准；3. 在1D、2D不规则三角网格和3D环面域中评估数值方法；4. 分析样本数量对SVD特征值估计和KL系数分布收敛的影响；5. 比较欧几里得距离与最短内部路径距离在非单连通拓扑中的效果。

Result: 1. 建立了模型驱动和数据驱动KLE构造的一致性理论框架；2. 数值实验验证了SVD方法在足够样本下的收敛性；3. 揭示了离散化策略、积分规则和样本数量之间的相互作用对KLE结果的影响；4. 在复杂几何和拓扑域中展示了KLE构造的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究为KLE的数值实现提供了统一的代数框架和基准测试方法，证明了基于样本的SVD方法与基于模型的Fredholm离散化方法在理论上的等价性，为复杂几何域和拓扑结构中的随机过程建模提供了实用指导。

Abstract: This report examines numerical aspects of constructing Karhunen-Loève expansions (KLEs) for second-order stochastic processes. The KLE relies on the spectral decomposition of the covariance operator via the Fredholm integral equation of the second kind, which is then discretized on a computational grid, leading to an eigendecomposition task. We derive the algebraic equivalence between this Fredholm-based eigensolution and the singular value decomposition of the weight-scaled sample matrix, yielding consistent solutions for both model-based and data-driven KLE construction. Analytical eigensolutions for exponential and squared-exponential covariance kernels serve as reference benchmarks to assess numerical consistency and accuracy in 1D settings. The convergence of SVD-based eigenvalue estimates and of the empirical distributions of the KL coefficients to their theoretical $\mathcal{N}(0,1)$ target are characterized as a function of sample count. Higher-dimensional configurations include a two-dimensional irregular domain discretized by unstructured triangular meshes with two refinement levels, and a three-dimensional toroidal domain whose non-simply-connected topology motivates a comparison between Euclidean and shortest interior path distances between the grid points. The numerical results highlight the interplay between the discretization strategy, quadrature rule, and sample count, and their impact on the KLE results.

</details>
