# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Bounded Representations by $x^2+y^2-z^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18087)
*Przemyslaw Chojecki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了每个充分大的整数n都可以表示为n=x²+y²-z²的形式，其中max(x²,y²,z²)≤n，解决了Erdős问题1148。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Erdős问题1148：证明每个充分大的整数n都可以表示为n=x²+y²-z²，且满足max(x²,y²,z²)≤n的条件。

Method: 将问题转化为在实双曲面b²-4ac=4n的固定相对紧开补丁中寻找正判别式4n的原始二元二次型，然后应用Duke定理的精确点计数形式（源自Einsiedler-Lindenstrauss-Michel-Venkatesh的测度论对偶性），最后通过有限奇偶性校正回到原始三元变量。

Result: 成功证明了每个充分大的整数n都可以表示为n=x²+y²-z²，且满足max(x²,y²,z²)≤n，从而完全解决了Erdős问题1148。

Conclusion: 通过将数论问题转化为几何问题并应用现代遍历理论和测度论方法，成功解决了Erdős提出的经典数论问题1148，展示了不同数学领域方法的交叉应用价值。

Abstract: We prove that every sufficiently large integer $n$ can be written in the form $n=x^2+y^2-z^2$ with $\textrm{max}(x^2,y^2,z^2)\le n$. The proof converts the problem into finding a primitive binary quadratic form of positive discriminant $4n$ inside a fixed relatively compact open patch of the real hyperboloid $b^2-4ac=4n$. This is then supplied by Duke's theorem in the precise point-counting form deduced from the measure-theoretic duality of Einsiedler-Lindenstrauss-Michel-Venkatesh. A finite parity correction returns to the original ternary variables. This settles Erdős Problem 1148.

</details>


### [95] [One one type of ud-preserving mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18121)
*Milan Pasteka*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在单位区间上构造了一类保持均匀分布的映射，其迭代序列形成Buck一致分布序列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单位区间上保持均匀分布的映射构造问题，探索其迭代序列的一致分布性质

Method: 在单位区间上构造一类特殊映射，证明这些映射保持均匀分布，并研究其迭代序列的Buck一致分布性质

Result: 成功构造了保持均匀分布的映射类，证明了其迭代序列具有Buck一致分布性质，并建立了这些映射的若干性质

Conclusion: 所构造的映射类在单位区间上保持均匀分布，其迭代序列形成Buck一致分布序列，为一致分布理论提供了新的构造方法

Abstract: In this paper a class of mappings on unit interval is constructed. These mapping preserve uniform distribution and theirs iterations form a sequence which is Buck uniformly distributed. In the third part some prorties of these mappings are proven.

</details>


### [96] [Why Eight Percent of Benford Sequences Never Converge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18243)
*James M. Hyman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该研究分析了Benford序列b^n中多位数相关性，使用条件互信息测量依赖性，通过连分数展开将基数分类为收敛和持久两种状态，发现约5.3%的基数表现出持久相关性，并证明了CMI偏差的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Benford序列b^n中多位数之间的相关性，特别是探索不同基数下这些相关性的持久性模式，以理解数字分布中的依赖结构。

Method: 使用条件互信息测量多位数相关性，通过连分数展开推导共振比来分类基数，对2到1000之间的996个基数进行数值分析，样本深度达到N=10,000和N=200,000，并通过2,988个测试案例验证理论边界。

Result: 在996个基数中，84个(8.4%)在N=10,000时表现出持久相关性，其中53个(5.3%)在N=200,000时确认为真正持久；证明了CMI偏差受分布误差限制，有效缩放为二次方；观察到收敛基数的有效指数为1.72±0.19。

Conclusion: Benford序列中的多位数相关性可以通过连分数展开分类，持久性基数的比例可能收敛于1/12，对于持久基数，收敛阈值在标准精度下超过10^6，使得渐近极限在计算范围内观测不到。

Abstract: We study multi-digit correlations in Benford sequences b^n for integer bases 2 <= b <= 1000, measuring dependence via conditional mutual information (CMI). A resonance ratio derived from the continued fraction expansion of log_10(b) classifies bases into convergent and persistent regimes (Theorem 3.13): among 996 bases surveyed, 84 (8.4%) exhibit persistent correlations at sample depth N = 10,000, and extended computation to N = 200,000 confirms 53 (5.3%) as genuinely persistent. We prove that CMI deviation is bounded by the distribution error (Theorem 3.4); exhaustive computation across 2,988 test cases confirms that the effective scaling is quadratic, yielding a two-sided rate beta = 2 for bounded-type bases (conditional on a computationally verified Hessian positivity condition). The observed effective exponent across 774 convergent bases is beta_eff = 1.72 +/- 0.19, consistent with finite-sample corrections to the asymptotic rate. We conjecture that the persistence rate converges to 1/12, a prediction grounded in the Gauss-Kuzmin distribution of partial quotients. For persistent bases, the convergence threshold N_epsilon exceeds 10^6 at standard precision, rendering the asymptotic limit observationally irrelevant within our computational scope.

</details>


### [97] [Norm-trace and Kloosterman sums in finite semi-simple algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18511)
*Daqing Wan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文将有限半单代数中给定迹和范数的元素计数公式从交换情形推广到非交换情形，得到了带平方根误差项的渐近公式，并应用于半单代数上的Kloosterman和估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限étale代数（交换情形）中给定迹和范数的元素计数结果推广到有限半单代数（非交换情形），建立更一般的理论框架。

Method: 应用一般线性群上的Eichler公式处理Gauss和，结合Hasse-Davenport关系，将问题约化到经典的几何情形（已知结果成立）。

Result: 获得了有限半单代数中给定迹和范数的元素计数的渐近公式，具有平方根误差项；作为约化的应用，得到了半单代数上Kloosterman和的平方根估计。

Conclusion: 成功将交换情形的计数结果推广到非交换的半单代数，建立了统一的渐近公式框架，并提出了关于乘积-迹计数的新猜想。

Abstract: An asymptotic formula with a square root error term is obtained for the number of elements with given trace and norm in a finite semisimple algebra over a finite field. This extends previous results from finite etale algebras (commutative case) to finite semi-simple algebras (non-commutative case). The main idea is to apply the Eichler formula for Gauss sums over the general linear group and the Hasse-Davenport relation to reduce the problem to the classical geometric case where the result is known to be true. As an application of this reduction, we also obtain a square root estimate for Kloosterman sums over semi-simple algebras. Similar square root estimates are discussed when norm-trace is replaced by product-trace, leading to a new conjecture on product-trace counting over finite semi-simple algebras.

</details>


### [98] [Close fields, affine Springer fibers and fundamental lemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18574)
*Sebastian Bartling,Kazuhiro Ito*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了仿射Springer纤维在相近局部域族中的几何局部常值定理，从而稳定轨道积分在这些族中局部常值，并将基变换基本引理和标准内窥基本引理从特征零推广到任意正特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射Springer纤维在局部域族中的行为，旨在将特征零的基本引理结果推广到正特征情形，解决数论和表示论中的重要问题。

Method: 通过证明仿射Springer纤维在相近局部域族中的几何局部常值定理，利用该几何结果推导稳定轨道积分的局部常值性。

Result: 稳定轨道积分在相近局部域族中局部常值，从而基变换基本引理和标准内窥基本引理可以从特征零情形转移到任意正特征情形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了仿射Springer纤维的几何局部常值定理，为基本引理在正特征情形的成立提供了理论基础，是Langlands纲领相关研究的重要进展。

Abstract: We prove a geometric local constancy theorem for affine Springer fibers in families of close local fields. Consequently, stable orbital integrals are locally constant in these families, and both the base change fundamental lemma and the standard endoscopic fundamental lemma transfer from characteristic zero to arbitrary positive characteristic.

</details>


### [99] [The triality of the twisted discrete trace formula for PGSO(8)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18763)
*Tuoping Du,Zhifeng Pen,Haoyang Wan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了PGSO(8)的三元性扭曲迹公式，包括其离散部分，并利用三元性性质获得了其自守表示的粗分类。通过将G_2的标准迹公式与PGSO(8)的三元性扭曲迹公式比较，推导出G_2自守表示的相应粗分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究PGSO(8)和G_2的自守表示分类问题。PGSO(8)具有特殊的三元性对称性，而G_2作为例外李群，其自守表示理论具有重要意义。通过迹公式方法，特别是利用PGSO(8)的三元性扭曲迹公式，可以建立这两个群的自守表示之间的对应关系。

Method: 1. 构造PGSO(8)的三元性扭曲椭圆内窥数据；2. 构造G_2的椭圆内窥数据；3. 基于这些构造和迹公式的一般框架，建立相关迹公式；4. 利用PGSO(8)的三元性性质，获得其自守表示的粗分类；5. 通过比较PGSO(8)的三元性扭曲迹公式和G_2的标准迹公式，推导G_2自守表示的粗分类。

Result: 成功建立了PGSO(8)的三元性扭曲迹公式（包括离散部分），获得了PGSO(8)自守表示的粗分类。通过比较迹公式，进一步获得了G_2自守表示的相应粗分类。

Conclusion: 本文通过迹公式方法，特别是利用PGSO(8)的三元性性质，建立了PGSO(8)和G_2自守表示的粗分类。这种方法展示了如何通过特殊对称群的迹公式来研究例外李群的自守表示理论，为相关领域的研究提供了新工具和视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the triality twisted trace formula for PGSO(8), including its discrete part, and obtain a coarse classification of its automorphic representations by combining the properties of triality. By comparing the standard trace formula for G_2 with the triality twisted trace formula for PGSO(8), we derive a corresponding coarse classification for automorphic representations of G_2. Specifically, we construct the triality-twisted elliptic endoscopic data for PGSO(8), and the elliptic endoscopic data for G_2. Based on these constructions and the general framework of trace formulas, we establish the relevant trace formulas. Utilizing the triality property of PGSO(8), we obtain a coarse classification of its automorphic representations, which in turn yields a coarse classification for those of G_2.

</details>


### [100] [Simplest cubic fields with small class number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18802)
*Akinari Hoshi,Hiroaki Iida*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过计算验证了最简单三次域类数的分布规律，确定了特定指数条件下类数≤1000的m值数量，并在大范围内统计了类数<16的具体分布情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最简单三次域L_m=ℚ(α)的类数分布规律，其中α是f_m(X)=X³-mX²-(m+3)X-1的根。旨在确定在不同指数条件[𝒪_{L_m}:ℤ[α]]下，类数h_m≤1000的整数m的数量，并分析在大范围m值内类数<16的具体分布。

Method: 使用PARI/GP计算工具进行数值计算和验证。首先考虑三种指数情况：[𝒪_{L_m}:ℤ[α]]=1,3,27，对应m²+3m+9=𝔣_m, 3𝔣_m, 27𝔣_m。在这些条件下，统计满足h_m≤1000的m≥-1的整数数量。其次，在-1≤m≤4×10⁶范围内，统计类数h_m<16的具体分布情况。

Result: 1. 当[𝒪_{L_m}:ℤ[α]]=1时，存在581个m≥-1使得h_m≤1000；当指数为3时，存在80个；当指数为27时，存在142个。
2. 在-1≤m≤4×10⁶范围内，h_m<16的m共有137个，具体分布为：h_m=1有26个，h_m=3有31个，h_m=4有10个，h_m=7有10个，h_m=9有36个，h_m=12有21个，h_m=13有3个。这些m值都被明确给出。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统计算揭示了最简单三次域类数的分布特征：在不同指数条件下，类数≤1000的m值数量存在显著差异（581、80、142）。在大范围搜索中，发现类数较小的域相对稀少，且类数分布呈现特定模式，为理解最简单三次域的算术性质提供了重要数值证据。

Abstract: Let $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ be an integer and $L_m=\mathbb{Q}(α)$ be the simplest cubic field with class number $h_m$ and conductor $\mathfrak{f}_m$ where $α$ is a root of $f_m(X)=X^3-mX^2-(m+3)X-1$. Let $\mathcal{O}_{L_m}$ be the ring of integers of $L_m$. By using PARI/GP, we confirm that if $[\mathcal{O}_{L_m}:\mathbb{Z}[α]]=1$ $($resp. $3$, $27$$)$, i.e. $m^2+3m+9=\mathfrak{f}_m$ $($resp. $3\mathfrak{f}_m$, $27\mathfrak{f}_m$$)$, then there exist exactly $581$ (resp. $80$, $142$) integers $m\geq -1$ such that $h_m\leq 1000$. We also show that if $-1\leq m\leq 4\cdot 10^6$, then $h_m<16$ holds for $137=26+31+10+10+36+21+3$ integers $m$. More precisely, there exist $26$ $($resp. $31$, $10$, $10$, $36$, $21$, $3$$)$ integers $m$ with $-1\leq m\leq 4\cdot 10^6$ such that $h_m=1$ $($resp. $3$, $4$, $7$, $9$, $12$, $13$$)$ which are given explicitly.

</details>


### [101] [Mahler's method and Carlitz logarithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18832)
*Guillaume Estienne*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为Papanikolas 2007年定理提供了新证明：若Carlitz对数在有理函数域上线性独立，则它们代数独立。新证明使用Mahler方法而非t-模理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Papanikolas在2007年使用t-模理论证明了Carlitz对数线性独立蕴含代数独立的定理。本文旨在提供基于Mahler方法的新证明，避免使用t-模理论，并扩展Denis 2006年的方法。

Method: 采用Mahler方法而非t-模理论。重新审视并扩展Denis 2006年针对Fq(theta)元素对数的Mahler系统方法，将其推广到更一般的Carlitz对数情况。

Result: 成功为Papanikolas定理提供了基于Mahler方法的新证明，验证了Carlitz对数线性独立蕴含代数独立的结论，扩展了Denis的方法适用范围。

Conclusion: Mahler方法可作为t-模理论的替代方案来证明Carlitz对数的代数独立性定理，Denis的方法可被推广到更一般情形，为相关研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: In 2007, Papanikolas established that if Carlitz logarithms of algebraic functions are linearly independent over the rational function field, then they are algebraically independent. The purpose of the present paper is to provide a new proof of this theorem using Mahler s method instead of the theory of t-motives. We revisit and extend the approach developed by Denis, which enabled him in 2006 to prove this result in the particular case of the logarithm of elements in Fq(theta) via a Mahler system.

</details>


### [102] [The non-abelian Leopoldt conjecture and equalities of $\mathcal{L}$-invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18961)
*Daniel Barrera Salazar,Andrew Graham,Chris Williams*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在非阿贝尔Leopoldt猜想下，证明了Hansen的p-adic超收敛上同调特征簇在"上同调重数为1"的非临界经典尖点处是étale的，并由此推导出Fontaine-Mazur和自守L-不变量的相等性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p-adic自守形式的几何性质，特别是特征簇的局部结构，并建立Fontaine-Mazur L-不变量与自守L-不变量之间的对应关系，验证Gehrmann猜想。

Method: 使用Hansen-Thorne的论证方法，在非阿贝尔Leopoldt猜想下分析p-adic超收敛上同调特征簇的局部étaleness性质；结合局部-整体兼容性结果，通过切向量假设推导L-不变量的相等性。

Result: 1. 在NALC下，Hansen的p-adic超收敛上同调特征簇在特定条件下是étale的；2. 在切向量假设下，证明了Fontaine-Mazur和自守L-不变量的相等性；3. 验证了对称幂提升情形的所有假设，得到了自守L-不变量的函子性结果。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔Leopoldt猜想蕴含了Gehrmann猜想：自守L-不变量与上同调度无关。该方法推广了Gehrmann-Rosso的工作，并为p-adic自守形式的几何研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a reductive group quasi-split at $p$. Using arguments of Hansen--Thorne, we show that under the non-abelian Leopoldt conjecture (NALC), Hansen's $p$-adic overconvergent cohomology eigenvariety for $G$ is étale over its image in weight space at any non-critical classical tempered cuspidal point of `cohomological multiplicity one'. This applies to all non-critical classical cuspidal points if $G = \mathrm{Res}_{F/\mathbb{Q}}\mathrm{GL}_n$.
  We then let $π$ be a $p$-ordinary regular algebraic cuspidal automorphic representation of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{Q}})$ such that $π_p$ is Steinberg. Combining the above étaleness result for the classical point attached to $π$, and a local-global compatibility result from our earlier work, we deduce -- under a tangent vector hypothesis that is true for at least half the simple roots -- the equality of Fontaine--Mazur and automorphic $\mathcal{L}$-invariants for $π$. Where this assumption is satisfied, we deduce the NALC implies a conjecture of Gehrmann: that automorphic $\mathcal{L}$-invariants are independent of cohomological degree. Our approach is inspired by (and generalises) previous work of Gehrmann--Rosso.
  When $π= \operatorname{Sym}^{n-1} π_f$ is the symmetric power lift of a modular form, we verify all assumptions other than the NALC, and deduce a functoriality result for the automorphic $\mathcal{L}$-invariants.

</details>


### [103] [Minimal Weierstrass models and regular models of hyperelliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19065)
*Qing Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究超椭圆曲线在离散赋值域上的极小Weierstrass模型，分析多模型情况下的正则模型和典范模型性质，对亏格2曲线用极小Weierstrass模型刻画稳定约化存在性，并利用特定模型计算雅可比行列式的Euler因子和Néron模型体积形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超椭圆曲线在离散赋值域上的极小Weierstrass模型，探索当存在多个极小Weierstrass模型时，这些模型与曲线的极小正则模型和典范模型之间的有趣关系。特别关注亏格2的情况，旨在通过极小Weierstrass模型来刻画稳定约化的存在性，并利用这些模型计算雅可比行列式的算术不变量。

Method: 在离散赋值域上研究超椭圆曲线的极小Weierstrass模型。当存在多个极小Weierstrass模型时，分析它们与极小正则模型和典范模型的关系。对于亏格2的曲线，通过极小Weierstrass模型来表征稳定约化的存在性。使用两个特定的极小Weierstrass模型来计算雅可比行列式的Euler因子和Néron模型的体积形式。

Result: 发现当存在多个极小Weierstrass模型时，它们与曲线的极小正则模型和典范模型之间存在有趣的性质联系。对于亏格2的曲线，成功用极小Weierstrass模型刻画了稳定约化的存在性。通过两个特定的极小Weierstrass模型，能够计算雅可比行列式的Euler因子和Néron模型的体积形式。

Conclusion: 极小Weierstrass模型在超椭圆曲线的算术几何研究中具有重要作用，特别是在存在多个模型的情况下。它们不仅与正则模型和典范模型有密切联系，还能用于刻画稳定约化性质并计算重要的算术不变量，为超椭圆曲线的算术研究提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Let $C$ be a hyperelliptic curve of genus $g\ge 2$ over a discrete valuation field $K$ with perfect residue field. We study the minimal Weierstrass models of $C$. When there is more than one such model, we find interesting properties on the minimal regular model and the canonical model of $C$. For curves of genus $2$, we characterize the existence of the stable reduction in terms of the minimal Weierstrass models. When there is more than one such model, we can compute the Euler factor of $\mathrm{Jac}(C)$ and a volume form of the Néron model of $\mathrm{Jac}(C)$, using two specific minimal Weierstrass models.

</details>


### [104] [Multiplication Tables for Integers with Restricted Prime Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19212)
*Jeremy Schlitt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究具有特定素数因子集合的整数在给定区间内存在除数的计数问题，推广了Ford的经典结果并发现了临界相变点


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ford在2008年研究了所有素因子都属于特定集合的整数在区间(y,2y]内存在除数的问题，但仅限于δ=1（即所有素数）的情况。本文旨在将这一结果推广到任意相对密度δ∈(0,1]的素数集合，并探索是否存在相变现象。

Method: 采用解析数论方法，通过建立计数函数的渐近公式来处理具有相对密度δ的素数集合。对于临界点δ=1/log4，使用更精细的分析技术来明确确定其行为特征。

Result: 对于所有δ∈(0,1]，确定了整数计数的数量级。特别发现当δ=1/log4时存在相变，并精确描述了该临界点的行为特征。这推广了Ford的δ=1结果，揭示了素数集合相对密度对整数除数分布的影响。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Ford关于整数除数计数的结果推广到任意相对密度的素数集合，并发现了在δ=1/log4处的相变现象。这一临界点的存在表明素数集合的相对密度对整数除数分布有重要影响，为理解整数因子结构的渐近行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a set of primes with relative density $δ$. We count integers in $[1,x]$ with prime factors all in $Q$ that also have a divisor in $(y,2y]$. We establish the order of magnitude for all $δ\in (0,1]$. This generalizes the case $δ= 1$ from the 2008 work of Ford. We also show that there is a phase transition at the critical point $δ= 1/\log 4$, for which we explicitly determine the behaviour.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Using Laplace Transform To Optimize the Hallucination of Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18022)
*Cheng Kang,Xinye Chen,Daniel Novak,Xujing Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文从控制理论视角将生成模型形式化为随机动力系统，通过拉普拉斯变换分析从根本上优化生成模型的幻觉问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索避免生成模型产生自信错误（幻觉）的可行性，从控制理论角度分析生成模型系统的功能和响应

Method: 将生成模型系统形式化为随机动力系统，利用控制理论分析系统函数和系统响应，通过拉普拉斯变换分析从宏观角度模拟源响应

Result: 发现训练进度与相应系统响应一致，这为开发更好的优化组件提供了有用途径，最终通过拉普拉斯变换分析从根本上优化了生成模型的幻觉问题

Conclusion: 从控制理论视角分析生成模型系统，通过拉普拉斯变换分析能够有效解决生成模型的幻觉问题，为优化组件开发提供了新思路

Abstract: To explore the feasibility of avoiding the confident error (or hallucination) of generation models (GMs), we formalise the system of GMs as a class of stochastic dynamical systems through the lens of control theory. Numerous factors can be attributed to the hallucination of the learning process of GMs, utilising knowledge of control theory allows us to analyse their system functions and system responses. Due to the high complexity of GMs when using various optimization methods, we cannot figure out their solution of Laplace transform, but from a macroscopic perspective, simulating the source response provides a virtual way to address the hallucination of GMs. We also find that the training progress is consistent with the corresponding system response, which offers us a useful way to develop a better optimization component. Finally, the hallucination problem of GMs is fundamentally optimized by using Laplace transform analysis.

</details>


### [106] [A Hybrid Decomposition Approach for Stochastic Unit Commitment with Combined-Cycle Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18177)
*Rosemary Barrass,Harsha Nagarajan,Mathieu Tanneau,Russell Bent,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合Benders分解和Dantzig-Wolfe分解算法，用于解决包含联合循环发电机(CCs)的随机机组组合问题，相比传统方法显著提高了求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国电网正经历重大范式转变，可再生能源、电动汽车和数据中心的发展增加了对快速响应发电机的需求。联合循环发电机(CCs)具有快速爬坡能力，但需要在机组组合问题中引入大量二元约束和变量，增加了求解难度。

Method: 提出混合Benders分解(BD)和Dantzig-Wolfe分解(DW)算法：利用BD分解线性约束，利用DW分解整数CC约束。算法结合了两种分解方法的优势，处理包含CCs的随机机组组合问题。

Result: 在修改后的935发电机FERC测试数据集上验证：相比传统BD在所有案例中均显示显著加速；在25个或更多场景的案例中，相比BD和Gurobi分支定界求解器具有更好的收敛率。

Conclusion: 该混合分解算法是解决随机机组组合问题的可扩展方法，特别适用于包含联合循环发电机的大规模随机优化问题，能够有效处理增加的整数约束。

Abstract: The U.S. power grid is undergoing a major paradigm shift with the increased development of renewable generators, electric vehicles, and data centers. In response to this growing need, the U.S. has ramped up the construction of combined-cycle generators (CCs). CCs are fast-ramping generators that utilize variable configurations of combustion turbines (CTs) and steam turbines (STs) to achieve much higher efficiency than traditional CTs alone. For schedule optimization, this requires the addition of a large number of binary constraints and variables in Unit Commitment (UC) problem formulations. This paper presents a novel hybrid Benders' (BD) and Dantzig-Wolfe (DW) decomposition algorithm for stochastic UC problems with CCs. The algorithm exploits the separability of the linear constraints in UC through BD and the integer CC constraints through DW. Results are presented for the 935-generator FERC test data set, modified to include mode data for CCs. The algorithm demonstrates a significant speed-up over traditional BD across all cases. It also demonstrates better convergence rates on cases with 25 or more scenarios than both BD and Gurobi's branch-and-bound solver. These cases show that the proposed algorithm is a scalable approach for solving stochastic UC.

</details>


### [107] [Solving Sparsity Constrained PCA, Regression, and QCQP via the Spartrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18215)
*Diego Cifuentes,Zhuorui Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于新型凸锥（spartrahedron）的凸松弛框架，用于解决稀疏二次约束二次规划问题，该框架能提供全局最优性证书，并在稀疏PCA、稀疏回归等应用中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏性在统计学、信号处理和数据科学中是基本建模原则，但带有稀疏约束的优化问题非常困难。现有的方法在处理稀疏二次约束二次规划问题时缺乏有效的凸松弛框架和全局最优性保证。

Method: 引入了一种新的凸松弛框架，基于一个名为spartrahedron的新型凸锥，该锥在矩阵层面精确刻画稀疏性。该方法将稀疏QCQP问题转化为半定规划松弛，当解为秩一时松弛是紧的，从而提供全局最优性证书。

Result: 建立了理论保证，包括稀疏PCA和稀疏岭回归的近似界和精确性区域，以及扰动下的稳定性结果。数值实验在稀疏PCA、稀疏回归、RIP常数估计和稀疏典型相关分析中验证了方法的实际效果。

Conclusion: 提出的基于spartrahedron的凸松弛框架为稀疏QCQP问题提供了有效的解决方案，具有理论保证和实际应用价值，能够处理包括稀疏回归、稀疏PCA在内的多种稀疏优化问题。

Abstract: Sparsity is a fundamental modeling principle in statistics, signal processing, and data science. However, optimization with sparsity constraints is notoriously difficult. We introduce a new convex relaxation framework for {sparse quadratically constrained quadratic programs} (QCQPs), a class that subsumes sparse regression, sparse principal component analysis (PCA), and related problems. Our approach is based on a novel convex cone, the spartrahedron, which exactly characterizes sparsity at the matrix level. This leads to a semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation that is tight whenever its solution is rank-one, providing a simple certificate of global optimality. We establish theoretical guarantees, including approximation bounds and exactness regions for sparse PCA and sparse ridge regression, as well as a general stability result under perturbations. Numerical experiments on sparse PCA, sparse regression, RIP constant estimation, and sparse canonical correlation analysis (CCA) demonstrate the practical success of our methods.

</details>


### [108] [Adversarial Robustness for Matrix Control Barrier Functions in Sampled-Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18307)
*James Usevitch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了在采样数据系统中使用矩阵控制屏障函数保证多智能体集合不变性的新理论结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多智能体系统中如何保证安全集合的不变性，特别是在存在对抗性智能体试图将系统状态驱动到不安全集合的情况下，以及如何处理相对于矩阵值安全集合函数具有高阶相对度的系统

Method: 1. 为异构控制仿射智能体应用零阶保持控制输入时计算控制输入以渲染由矩阵不等式定义的安全集合前向不变的条件；2. 引入在考虑对抗性智能体存在时保证集合不变性的方法；3. 将这些集合不变性结果理论扩展到相对于矩阵值安全集合函数具有高阶相对度的系统

Result: 提出了保证多智能体集合不变性的理论条件，包括在对抗性智能体存在下的鲁棒性保证，以及扩展到高阶相对度系统的理论框架

Conclusion: 该论文为采样数据多智能体系统提供了基于矩阵控制屏障函数的集合不变性保证的理论基础，能够处理异构智能体、对抗性智能体和高阶相对度系统，扩展了多智能体安全控制的理论工具

Abstract: This paper presents novel theoretical results to guarantee multi-agent set invariance using Matrix Control Barrier Functions in sampled-data systems. More specifically, the paper presents conditions under which heterogeneous control-affine agents applying zero-order-hold control inputs can compute control inputs to render safe sets defined by matrix inequalities forward invariant. It then introduces methods to guarantee set invariance while accounting for the presence of adversarial agents seeking to drive the system state to unsafe sets. Finally, the paper presents theoretical extensions of these set invariance results to systems having high relative degree with respect to the matrix-valued safe set function.

</details>


### [109] [Stabilization of highly nonlinear hybrid stochastic differential delay equations by periodically intermittent feedback controls based on discrete-time observations with asynchronous switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18367)
*Guangqiang Lan,Fansai Meng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程的矩指数镇定，设计基于离散时间状态观测的异步切换周期间歇控制器，获得观测周期上界和控制宽度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程，需要设计有效的控制器实现系统的矩指数稳定。由于实际系统中状态观测通常是离散的且存在异步切换，需要研究基于离散时间状态观测的周期间歇控制方法。

Method: 设计基于离散时间状态观测的异步切换周期间歇控制器。首先在广义Khasminskii型条件下建立解p阶矩的有穷性和有界性，然后给出控制函数、漂移系数和扩散系数的合理条件，证明受控系统的指数稳定性及收敛速率。

Result: 获得了观测周期的上界和控制宽度的下界，证明了系统的指数稳定性并给出了收敛速率。通过实例表明控制区间比例与收敛速率呈正相关关系。

Conclusion: 提出的基于离散时间状态观测的异步切换周期间歇控制器能有效实现高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程的矩指数镇定，控制区间比例越大，系统收敛越快。

Abstract: In this paper, we will investigate the moment exponential stabilization of highly nonlinear hybrid stochastic differential delay equations. A periodically intermittent controller based on discrete time state observations with asynchronous switching is designed. The upper bound of observation period as well as the lower bound of the control width are all obtained. Firstly, the finiteness and boundedness of the $p$-th moment of the solution are established under a generalized Khasminskii-type condition. Then reasonable conditions of control function, drift and diffusion coefficients are presented. Then exponential stability as well as the convergence rate of controlled system are proved. Finally, an example is presented to interpret the conclusion, which also indicates that the proportion of control interval has positive relation to the convergence rate.

</details>


### [110] [Fundamental Limits for Sensor-Based Control via the Gibbs Variational Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18454)
*Vincent Pacelli,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于吉布斯变分原理的自洽下界方法，用于计算部分观测下因果反馈控制器的最小期望成本，解决了现有信息论方法在反馈最有价值时性能退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈控制器性能的基本极限对于算法基准测试、传感器选择和任务可行性认证至关重要，但现有通用计算工具很少。传统信息论方法通过评估传感器相对于无控制系统的性能来高估所需信息，导致在反馈最有价值时性能界限反而退化。

Method: 通过将吉布斯变分原理应用于状态和观测的联合路径测度，推导出部分观测下任何因果反馈控制器的最小期望成本下界。该方法具有自洽细化特性：好的控制器会集中状态分布，从而限制传感器可提取的信息，进而收紧界限。通过二分法求解固定点方程，并在自由能最小化可证明凸的条件下提供可认证正确的数值界限。

Result: 在非线性Dubins汽车跟踪问题上，自洽界限能够捕捉大部分最优成本（跨传感器噪声水平），而无控制变体在低噪声下变得无效。固定点方程有唯一解，可通过二分法计算，并在自由能最小化可证明凸的条件下提供可认证正确的数值界限。

Conclusion: 该方法为非线性、非完整和混合动力学系统（具有无界成本）的反馈控制器性能提供了实用的下界计算工具，解决了现有方法在反馈最有价值时性能退化的问题，并通过自洽细化机制实现了更紧的界限估计。

Abstract: Fundamental limits on the performance of feedback controllers are essential for benchmarking algorithms, guiding sensor selection, and certifying task feasibility -- yet few general-purpose tools exist for computing them. Existing information-theoretic approaches overestimate the information a sensor must provide by evaluating it against the uncontrolled system, producing bounds that degrade precisely when feedback is most valuable. We derive a lower bound on the minimum expected cost of any causal feedback controller under partial observations by applying the Gibbs variational principle to the joint path measure over states and observations. The bound applies to nonlinear, nonholonomic, and hybrid dynamics with unbounded costs and admits a self-consistent refinement: any good controller concentrates the state, which limits the information the sensor can extract, which tightens the bound. The resulting fixed-point equation has a unique solution computable by bisection, and we provide conditions under which the free energy minimization is provably convex, yielding a certifiably correct numerical bound. On a nonlinear Dubins car tracking problem, the self-consistent bound captures most of the optimal cost across sensor noise levels, while the open-loop variant is vacuous at low noise.

</details>


### [111] [Axis-Aligned Relaxations for Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18458)
*Haisheng Zhu,Taotao He,Mohit Tawarmalani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于计算几何的混合整数非线性规划松弛框架，通过凸化策略选取的点集构建多面体松弛，迭代逼近函数图的同步凸包，在多项式和MINLPLib实例上显著提升最优间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合整数非线性规划松弛方法存在局限性，标准可分解和复合松弛方案不够紧致，需要更精确的凸松弛方法来提升求解效率和边界质量。

Method: 基于计算几何构建多面体松弛框架：1) 提出新的显式不等式类，改进函数乘积的松弛；2) 证明轴对齐区域上多线性函数的同步凸包完全由角点值确定；3) 集成体素化和QuickHull等计算几何工具高效逼近可行域和函数图。

Result: 在随机生成多项式优化问题和619个MINLPLib实例上评估：多项式实例中，半数实例的最优间隙比标准方法额外缩小20-25%；与增强可分解规划基线和Gurobi根节点边界相比，在约30%的MINLPLib实例上获得更优对偶边界，约10%实例的间隙减少超过50%。

Conclusion: 提出的计算几何松弛框架显著提升了混合整数非线性规划松弛的紧致性，通过新的不等式类、理论证明和高效几何工具集成，为MINLP求解提供了更精确的边界近似。

Abstract: We present a novel relaxation framework for general mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) grounded in computational geometry. Our approach constructs polyhedral relaxations by convexifying finite sets of strategically chosen points, iteratively refining the approximation to converge toward the simultaneous convex hull of factorable function graphs. The framework is underpinned by three key contributions: (i) a new class of explicit inequalities for products of functions that strictly improve upon standard factorable and composite relaxation schemes; (ii) a proof establishing that the simultaneous convex hull of multilinear functions over axis-aligned regions is fully determined by their values at corner points, thereby generalizing existing results from hypercubes to arbitrary axis-aligned domains; and (iii) the integration of computational geometry tools, specifically voxelization and QuickHull, to efficiently approximate feasible regions and function graphs. We implement this framework and evaluate it on randomly generated polynomial optimization problems and a suite of 619 instances from \texttt{MINLPLib}. Numerical results demonstrate significant improvements over state-of-the-art benchmarks: on polynomial instances, our relaxation closes an additional 20--25\% of the optimality gap relative to standard methods on half the instances. Furthermore, compared against an enhanced factorable programming baseline and Gurobi's root-node bounds, our approach yields superior dual bounds on approximately 30\% of \texttt{MINLPLib} instances, with roughly 10\% of cases exhibiting a gap reduction exceeding 50\%.

</details>


### [112] [Modeling Adversarial Wildfires for Power Grid Disruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18473)
*Matthew Brun,Xu Andy Sun,Jean-Paul Watson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出混合整数锥规划模型描述针对电力基础设施的对抗性野火，基于Rothermel火势蔓延模型设计风助火势蔓延集，并开发凸松弛方法，应用于加州实际火灾案例评估电力系统韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力基础设施面临日益严重的野火破坏风险，但传统野火模型无法有效描述最坏情况或高影响火灾行为，需要开发能够表征针对基础设施的对抗性野火模型。

Method: 提出混合整数锥规划模型，基于Rothermel火势蔓延模型设计风助火势蔓延集，开发新的欧几里得球内积凸松弛方法，构建安全约束最优潮流模型提升运行韧性。

Result: 应用于加州Park、Eaton和Palisades火灾案例，识别多元素故障的最小停电时间，评估由现实野火引起的元件停运序列的最大负荷削减，构建安全约束最优潮流模型。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效表征针对电力基础设施的对抗性野火，为野火易发地区电网运营商提供评估和提升系统韧性的实用工具，通过最小停电时间筛选故障并构建韧性运行策略。

Abstract: Electric power infrastructure faces increasing risk of damage and disruption due to wildfire. Operators of power grids in wildfire-prone regions must consider the potential impacts of unpredictable fires. However, traditional wildfire models do not effectively describe worst-case, or even high-impact, fire behavior. To address this issue, we propose a mixed-integer conic program to characterize an adversarial wildfire that targets infrastructure while respecting realistic fire spread dynamics. We design a wind-assisted fire spread set based on the Rothermel fire spread model and propose principled convex relaxations of this set, including a new relaxation of the inner product over Euclidean balls. We present test cases derived from the recent Park, Eaton, and Palisades fires in California and solve models to identify the minimum time-to-outage of multiple-element contingencies and the maximum load shed associated with a sequence of element outages caused by a realistic wildfire. We use the minimum time-to-outage values to screen contingencies and construct security-constrained optimal power flow models that promote operational resilience against wildfire.

</details>


### [113] [Computationally Efficient Density-Driven Optimal Control via Analytical KKT Reduction and Contractive MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18503)
*Julian Martinez,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种将多智能体密度驱动最优控制中的大规模KKT系统转化为紧凑二次规划的方法，实现线性计算复杂度，显著降低在线计算负担


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密度驱动最优控制（D2OC）框架在实现预测控制时需要求解大规模KKT系统，其计算复杂度随预测时域呈立方增长，限制了大规模多智能体系统的实时应用

Method: 提出解析结构约简方法，将T时域KKT系统转化为紧凑二次规划（QP），实现O(T)线性可扩展性；引入收缩性Lyapunov约束，证明闭环系统对参考传播漂移的输入到状态稳定性（ISS）

Result: 方法显著降低计算负担，相比传统O(T³)方法实现线性可扩展性；数值仿真验证了快速密度覆盖能力和计算加速效果，支持大规模多智能体群的长时域预测控制

Conclusion: 提出的结构约简方法有效解决了密度驱动最优控制的计算瓶颈，通过线性可扩展的QP公式和稳定性保证，实现了大规模多智能体系统的高效协调控制

Abstract: Efficient coordination for collective spatial distribution is a fundamental challenge in multi-agent systems. Prior research on Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) established a framework to match agent trajectories to a desired spatial distribution. However, implementing this as a predictive controller requires solving a large-scale Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) system, whose computational complexity grows cubically with the prediction horizon. To resolve this, we propose an analytical structural reduction that transforms the T-horizon KKT system into a condensed quadratic program (QP). This formulation achieves O(T) linear scalability, significantly reducing the online computational burden compared to conventional O(T^3) approaches. Furthermore, to ensure rigorous convergence in dynamic environments, we incorporate a contractive Lyapunov constraint and prove the Input-to-State Stability (ISS) of the closed-loop system against reference propagation drift. Numerical simulations verify that the proposed method facilitates rapid density coverage with substantial computational speed-up, enabling long-horizon predictive control for large-scale multi-agent swarms.

</details>


### [114] [Optimal strategies for controlled growth in metastable Kawasaki dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18903)
*Simone Baldassarri,Maike C. de Jongh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为二维正方晶格中Kawasaki动力学下的低温亚稳态Ising模型建立了马尔可夫决策过程框架，研究外部控制器如何通过适时添加和移动粒子引导系统达到全占据态，并分析了两种奖励结构下的最优策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在低温亚稳态Ising模型中，如何通过外部控制策略有效引导系统从初始状态达到目标的全占据态，这对于理解非平衡统计物理中的控制问题具有重要意义。

Method: 建立马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)框架，将系统建模为受控随机过程；构建约束于单簇构型空间的简化MDP；分析两种奖励结构：仅依赖到达目标时间的效率型奖励，以及包含动作相关能量成本的能耗型奖励。

Result: 在MDP框架下精确刻画了两种奖励结构的最优策略：纯效率标准促进从簇边界中心生长，而能量奖励函数则偏好从簇角落生长，表明不同优化目标导致不同的生长模式。

Conclusion: 外部控制器可以通过不同的最优策略引导Ising系统达到全占据态，具体策略取决于优化目标（效率vs能量成本），这为理解非平衡系统中的控制问题提供了理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a Markov decision process (MDP) formulation for the low--temperature metastable Ising model evolving according to Kawasaki dynamics in a finite box of the two--dimensional square lattice. We analyze how an external controller can guide the system to the all--occupied state by appropriately adding and moving particles at specified moments in time. To this end, we construct a reduced MDP on a constrained family of configurations having a single cluster, a regime where particle attachment is more likely than detachment. We investigate two reward structures: one that depends solely on the time to reach the target configuration, and another that incorporates action--dependent energy costs. Within this MDP framework, we characterize the exact optimal policies under both reward structures, which turn out to have a different behavior: while a purely efficiency--based criterion promotes the growth from the boundary centers of the cluster, an energy--based reward function favours the growth at the corners of the cluster.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Decision-Sufficient Representations for Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18551)
*Yuhan Ye,Saurabh Amin,Asuman Ozdauglar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性规划中决策充分数据集(SDD)的构造问题，证明了相关计算问题是NP难的，并提出了多项式时间的近似算法和压缩方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然提供了决策充分数据集(SDD)的几何特征，但其构造算法需要求解混合整数规划，计算复杂。本文旨在解决这一计算难题，为实际应用提供高效的构造方法。

Method: 1) 证明计算决策相关维度d*是NP难的，判断数据集全局充分性是coNP难的；2) 引入逐点充分性作为松弛概念；3) 在非退化条件下，提出多项式时间的割平面算法构造逐点充分决策数据集；4) 在i.i.d.成本数据驱动场景下，提出累积算法聚合决策相关方向；5) 将决策充分表示应用于上下文线性优化。

Result: 1) 建立了计算复杂性的下界；2) 提出了多项式时间的构造算法；3) 获得了分布无关的PAC保证：逐点充分性失败概率上界为Õ(d*/n)；4) 在上下文线性优化中，获得了Õ(√(d*/n))的泛化界，优于传统的Õ(√(d/n))。

Conclusion: 本文解决了决策充分数据集构造的计算难题，证明了相关问题的NP难性，并提出了实用的多项式时间算法。通过逐点充分性松弛和累积压缩方案，实现了高效的决策数据集构造，并在上下文线性优化中获得了维度依赖的泛化改进。

Abstract: We study how to construct compressed datasets that suffice to recover optimal decisions in linear programs with an unknown cost vector $c$ lying in a prior set $\mathcal{C}$. Recent work by Bennouna et al. provides an exact geometric characterization of sufficient decision datasets (SDDs) via an intrinsic decision-relevant dimension $d^\star$. However, their algorithm for constructing minimum-size SDDs requires solving mixed-integer programs. In this paper, we establish hardness results showing that computing $d^\star$ is NP-hard and deciding whether a dataset is globally sufficient is coNP-hard, thereby resolving a recent open problem posed by Bennouna et al. To address this worst-case intractability, we introduce pointwise sufficiency, a relaxation that requires sufficiency for an individual cost vector. Under nondegeneracy, we provide a polynomial-time cutting-plane algorithm for constructing pointwise-sufficient decision datasets. In a data-driven regime with i.i.d.\ costs, we further propose a cumulative algorithm that aggregates decision-relevant directions across samples, yielding a stable compression scheme of size at most $d^\star$. This leads to a distribution-free PAC guarantee: with high probability over the training sample, the pointwise sufficiency failure probability on a fresh draw is at most $\tilde{O}(d^\star/n)$, and this rate is tight up to logarithmic factors. Finally, we apply decision-sufficient representations to contextual linear optimization, obtaining compressed predictors with generalization bounds scaling as $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d^\star/n})$ rather than $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d/n})$, where $d$ is the ambient cost dimension.

</details>


### [116] [Robust mean-field games under entropy-based uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18628)
*François Delarue,Pierre Lavigne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一类新的熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈问题，其中代表性智能体与自然对抗，通过min-max随机控制问题建模，自然以熵成本扭曲参考概率测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在不确定性环境下，当智能体面临自然（作为对手）对概率测度的扰动时，如何建立鲁棒的平均场博弈框架，扩展经典平均场博弈理论以处理模型不确定性。

Method: 1) 构建熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈模型，其中智能体目标为min-max随机控制问题，自然以熵成本扭曲参考概率测度；2) 使用Schauder不动点定理证明平均场博弈均衡存在性；3) 引入联合平坦反单调性和位移单调性条件确保唯一性；4) 提出两类N-玩家游戏，证明平均场博弈极限产生ε-纳什均衡。

Result: 1) 建立了熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈均衡的存在性；2) 在联合平坦反单调性和位移单调性条件下证明了均衡的唯一性；3) 展示了该平均场博弈模型可作为两类N-玩家游戏的极限，产生ε-纳什均衡。

Conclusion: 成功构建并分析了熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈框架，扩展了经典Lasry-Lions单调性理论，为处理模型不确定性的平均场博弈问题提供了理论保证和实际应用基础。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce a new class of entropy-penalized robust mean field game problems in which the representative agent is opposed to Nature. The agent's objective is formulated as a min-max stochastic control problem, in which Nature distorts the reference probability measure at an entropic cost. As a consequence, the distribution of the continuum of agents represented by the player is given by the effective measure induced by Nature. Existence of a mean-field game equilibrium is established via a Schauder fixed point argument. To ensure uniqueness, we introduce a joint flat anti-monotonicity and displacement monotonicity condition, extending the classical Lasry-Lions monotonicity framework. Finally, we present two classes of N -player games for which the mean-field game limit yields $ε$-Nash equilibria.

</details>


### [117] [Robust Discrete Pricing Optimization via Multiple-Choice Knapsack Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18653)
*Zi Yuan Eric Shao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究离散投资组合定价问题，在利润和公平约束下从有限价格菜单中选择最优价格，采用预算鲁棒优化处理需求不确定性，通过MCKP转化和结构分析获得精确解法和有界积分间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散投资组合定价问题，需要在考虑需求不确定性的情况下，从有限价格菜单中选择最优定价策略，同时满足利润边际和公平性约束，以在实际商业应用中实现鲁棒优化。

Method: 采用预算鲁棒优化框架控制保守性，将问题转化为多选择背包问题(MCKP)，分析LP松弛的结构特性（上包络过滤和包络段贪婪填充），为固定参数子问题提供精确LP松弛解法，证明积分间隙的有界性。

Result: 证明固定参数子问题的积分间隙以单项目包络跳跃为界，相对间隙在标准有界性和线性增长假设下以O(1/n)衰减；数值实验表明鲁棒边际保护可实现低于1%的名义收入损失。

Conclusion: 该研究为离散投资组合定价问题提供了计算可行的鲁棒优化方法，通过结构分析和理论保证，在实际应用中能够有效平衡需求不确定性和收入目标。

Abstract: We study a discrete portfolio pricing problem that selects one price per product from a finite menu under margin and fairness constraints. To account for demand uncertainty, we incorporate a budgeted robust formulation that controls conservatism while remaining computationally tractable. By reducing the problem to a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), we identify structural properties of the LP relaxation, in particular upper-hull filtering and greedy filling over hull segments, that yield an exact solution method for the LP relaxation of the fixed-parameter subproblems. For the resulting fixed-parameter subproblems, we show that the integrality gap is bounded additively by a single-item hull jump, and that the corresponding relative gap decays as O(1/n) under standard boundedness and linear-growth assumptions. Numerical experiments on synthetic portfolios and a stylized retail case study with economically calibrated parameters are consistent with these bounds and indicate that robust margin protection can be achieved with less than 1 percent nominal revenue loss on the instances tested.

</details>


### [118] [Stabilization of Integral Difference Equations by solving a Corona problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18703)
*Adam Braun,Jean Auriol,Lucas Brivadis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对具有点状和分布输入延迟的积分差分方程系统的稳定状态反馈控制律，通过积分算子表达控制器，基于Corona问题的卷积方程求解控制器核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDE（积分差分方程）稳定化方法存在局限性，需要扩展至更一般的设置，允许任意数量的点状延迟同时影响状态和输入，且无需满足可公度性假设。

Method: 提出通过积分算子作用于状态和输入历史的控制器表达形式；将控制器核函数表征为Corona问题产生的卷积方程的解；在谱可稳定条件下确保解的存在性；采用最小二乘程序进行数值求解。

Result: 建立了闭环稳定性，证明了在合适的谱可稳定条件下控制器核函数解的存在性，并实现了数值求解方法，扩展了现有IDE稳定化结果。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将IDE稳定化扩展到更一般的设置，允许任意数量的点状延迟同时影响状态和输入，无需可公度性假设，为具有延迟的向量值状态系统提供了有效的稳定控制策略。

Abstract: This paper proposes a stabilizing state-feedback control law for vector-valued state systems with a scalar control input, governed by a general class of integral difference equations that incorporate both pointwise and distributed input delays. The proposed controller is expressed through integral operators acting on the state and input histories over a finite time horizon. Closed-loop stability is established by characterizing the controller kernels as solutions to a convolution equation arising from a Corona problem. The existence of such solutions is ensured under a suitable spectral stabilizability condition, and a least-square procedure is implemented to find them numerically. The approach extends existing IDE stabilization results to more general settings, allowing for arbitrary numbers of pointwise delays affecting both the state and input, without requiring commensurability assumptions.

</details>


### [119] [A mathematical framework for time-delay reservoir computing analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18706)
*Anh-Tuan Clabaut,Jean Auriol,Islam Boussaada,Guilherme Mazanti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为时延储备池计算提供了控制理论框架，建立了分离性、鲁棒性和渐近记忆的严格数学基础，并通过傅里叶分析为线性储备池推导出分离距离的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备池计算虽然实验进展显著，但其核心特性（分离性、鲁棒性和渐近记忆）仍缺乏严格的数学理论基础，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出基于控制理论的时延储备池分析框架，使用泛函范数形式化定义分离性和渐近记忆，建立与时延系统增量输入到状态稳定性的联系，并通过傅里叶分析为线性储备池推导分离距离的显式下界。

Result: 建立了储备池计算特性的严格数学基础，为线性储备池提供了可计算的分离距离下界，在NARMA10基准测试和连续时间系统预测中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该控制理论框架为储备池计算提供了坚实的数学基础，推导的分离距离下界为储备池设计提供了可计算的准则，最小化数字实现验证了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Reservoir computing is a well-established approach for processing data with a much lower complexity compared to traditional neural networks. Despite two decades of experimental progress, the core properties of reservoir computing (namely separation, robustness, and fading memory) still lack rigorous mathematical foundations. This paper addresses this gap by providing a control-theoretic framework for the analysis of time-delay-based reservoir computers. We introduce formal definitions of the separation property and fading memory in terms of functional norms, and establish their connection to well-known stability notions for time-delay systems as incremental input-to-state stability. For a class of linear reservoirs, we derive an explicit lower bound for the separation distance via Fourier analysis, offering a computable criterion for reservoir design. Numerical results on the NARMA10 benchmark and continuous-time system prediction validate the approach with a minimal digital implementation.

</details>


### [120] [Non-linear in-band interference cancellation on base of conjugate gradients method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18727)
*Alexander Degtyarev,Sergei Bakhurin,Nikita Yudin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法，用于全双工通信系统中多层非线性自干扰消除器的自适应优化，在收敛速度和计算资源需求之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工通信系统中的自干扰消除问题，多层非线性模型自适应优化时存在局部极小值和鞍点问题，二阶方法因海森矩阵不定性而受限，混合牛顿法计算资源需求过高。

Method: 提出基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法，利用损失函数的混合二阶导数信息而不显式计算完整海森矩阵，在数字域执行自干扰消除。

Result: 所提方法比一阶方法具有更高的收敛速度，同时比传统二阶方法需要显著更少的计算资源，适用于多层非线性自干扰消除器的自适应优化。

Conclusion: 基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法为全双工通信系统中的自干扰消除提供了一种有效的优化方案，在收敛性能和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates one possible solution to the problem of self-interference cancellation (SIC) arising in the design of in-band full-duplex (IBFD) communication systems. Self-interference cancellation is performed in the digital domain using multilayer nonlinear models adapted via gradient-based optimization. The presence of local minima and saddle points during the adaptation of multilayer models limits the direct use of second-order methods due to the indefiniteness of the hessian matrix. The mixed Newton method can address the saddle-point issue; however, it requires significant computational resources. In this work, a conjugate gradient (CG) method constructed on the base of the mixed Newton method (MNM) is proposed. The method exploits information from mixed second-order derivatives of the loss function without explicit computation the full hessian matrix. As a result, the proposed approach achieves a higher convergence rate than first-order methods while requiring significantly lower computational resources than conventional second-order methods when adapting multilayer nonlinear self-interference cancellers in full-duplex communication systems.

</details>


### [121] [Scheduling Ground-Based Telescope Observations with Uncertain Nights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18730)
*Thomas Rahab Lacroix,Pierre Lemaire,Anne-Marie Lagrange,Julien Milli,Nadia Brauner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于随机优化和反应策略的数学方法，用于在天气不确定条件下调度地面望远镜观测，以提高观测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面望远镜观测成本高昂（如ESO每晚8.3万欧元），且受气象条件（云、风、大气湍流）影响严重。在不确定性存在的情况下，准确的观测调度能显著提高望远镜使用效率，需要自动化工具支持。

Method: 采用基于随机优化和反应策略的数学方法，建立考虑多个观测目标、不确定的晴朗夜晚数量以及各种观测约束的模型。

Result: 通过与其他方法比较，证明了基于随机优化和反应策略方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 随机优化和反应策略为地面望远镜在天气不确定条件下的观测调度提供了有效的数学方法，能够显著提高望远镜使用效率。

Abstract: The observation of celestial objects is a fundamental activity in astronomy. Ground-based and space telescopes are used to gather electromagnetic radiation from space, allowing astronomers to study a wide range of celestial objects and phenomena, such as stars, planets, galaxies, and black holes. The European Southern Observatory (ESO) charges each night 83 kEUR (Milli et al. 2019), so the schedules of the telescopes are really important in order to optimize every second. Ground-based telescopes are affected by meteorological conditions, such as clouds, wind, and atmospheric turbulence. Accurate scheduling of observations in the presence of such uncertainties can significantly improve the efficiency of telescopes use and support from automated tools is highly desirable. In this paper, we study a mathematical approach for scheduling ground-based telescope observations under an uncertain number of clear nights due to uncertain weather and atmospheric conditions. The model considers multiple targets, uncertain number of nights, and various observing constraints. We demonstrate the viability and effectiveness of an approach based on stochastic optimization and reactive strategy comparing it against other methods.

</details>


### [122] [Second-Order Conditions for Infinite-Horizon Semilinear Parabolic Control Problems without Tikhonov Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18745)
*Eduardo Casas,Nicolai Jork*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究无限时域下半线性抛物型最优控制问题，无Tikhonov正则化，考虑Neumann边界条件、控制约束，建立二阶充分最优性条件并证明有限时域解收敛于无限时域解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限时域半线性抛物最优控制问题，主要挑战在于目标函数中不包含Tikhonov正则化项，这使得经典有限时域理论难以直接扩展到无限时域问题。需要克服无正则化带来的理论困难，建立无限时域问题的适定性理论。

Method: 考虑Neumann边界条件下的半线性抛物方程，控制约束在时间上是逐点的，在空间上可以是逐点或积分约束。采用无Tikhonov正则化的成本泛函，通过分析有限时域问题到无限时域问题的极限行为，建立二阶充分最优性条件。

Result: 建立了无限时域半线性抛物最优控制问题的二阶充分最优性条件，证明了当时间域趋于无穷时，有限时域问题的局部最优状态收敛于无限时域问题的局部最优状态。

Conclusion: 成功克服了无Tikhonov正则化带来的理论困难，建立了无限时域半线性抛物最优控制问题的适定性理论，为这类问题的数值计算和分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider semilinear parabolic optimal control problems subject to Neumann boundary conditions, control constraints, and an infinite time horizon. The control constraints are pointwise in time, but they can be pointwise or integral in the space variable. Crucially, the optimal control problem does not include a Tikhonov regularization in the cost functional, which provides a major difficulty in the extension of the classical finite-horizon theory to infinite-horizon optimal control problems. As a consequence of our findings, we establish a sufficient second-order optimality condition and prove that local optimal states of the finite-horizon problems approximate local optimal states to the infinite-horizon problem as the horizon tends to infinity.

</details>


### [123] [The multi-objective portfolio model for oil and gas exploration drilling projects selection and its operator-enhanced NSGA-II based solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18844)
*Chao Min,Junyi Cui,Stanisław Migórski,Yonglan Xie,Qingxia Zhang,Jun Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个多目标均值-方差投资组合模型，用于油气勘探钻井投资组合优化，考虑了地质参数不确定性，并提出了改进的OE-NSGA-II算法来求解该模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 油气勘探钻井投资对运营规划至关重要。传统方法依赖碎片化的专家评估，信息整合能力有限。投资组合工具在缓解不确定性和选择优质钻井计划方面具有强大潜力，因此需要开发一个能够有效权衡风险回报的优化模型。

Method: 1. 通过专家先验获取前景项目地质参数的概率分布；2. 将钻井项目选择建模为投资组合优化问题，在不同约束下联合描述短期计划的回报和风险；3. 提出改进的OE-NSGA-II算法，包括：(1) 设计定向交叉算子，将目标空间中的改进方向嵌入重组过程；(2) 设计结构感知变异算子，通过概率采样和可行性修复优先考虑高效用比特翻转。

Result: 使用2023年勘探钻井部署案例进行验证，然后将验证后的方法应用于2024年部署以支持决策制定。结果表明，该方法为油气勘探钻井投资组合优化提供了可重复使用的解决方案。

Conclusion: 提出的多目标均值-方差投资组合模型和改进的OE-NSGA-II算法能够有效处理地质参数不确定性，实现风险回报权衡，为油气勘探钻井投资组合优化提供了系统化的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Drilling investment is pivotal to operational planning in oil and gas (O\&G) exploration. Conventional deployment relies heavily on fragmented expert assessments of geological and economic factors, with limited integration ability of information. As the tool of portfolio show strong potential for mitigating uncertainty and selecting superior drilling plans, this study develops a multi-objective mean-variance portfolio model that accounts for geological-parameter uncertainty, enabling an effective risk-return trade-off and optimal selection. First, the probabilistic distribution of geological-parameters for prospect-list projects is obtained through expert-elicited priors. And considering the selection of the drilling projects as a portfolio, an optimization model is formulated jointly to describe the return and risk of short-term plan, under different constraints. Second, an improved OE-NSGA-II algorithm is proposed specifically for this model, in which (1) a directional crossover operator is designed to embed improving directions in objective space-derived from dominance and objective differences-into recombination, and (2) a structure-aware mutation operator is designed to prioritize high-utility bit flips via probabilistic sampling with feasibility repair, thus improving the search ability for superior Pareto solutions. Finally, using the case of 2023 exploration drilling deployment for verification, and then apply the validated method to the 2024 deployment to support decision-making. The results indicate that the proposed approach offers a reusable solution for drilling portfolio optimization in O\&G exploration.

</details>


### [124] [A new optimal control algorithm for the Keller-Segel problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18889)
*F. Guillen-Gonzalez,F. Palmero-Ramos,M. A. Rodriguez-Bellido,G. Tierra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于Keller-Segel趋化系统的最优控制算法，同时考虑边界和分布控制，通过离散化状态问题、离散伴随方案计算梯度，使用Adam梯度下降法进行优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Keller-Segel趋化系统开发新的最优控制算法，该系统在生物学中描述细胞对化学信号的趋化行为。需要同时处理边界和分布控制，这些控制通过引入/移除系统中的化学物质量来实现

Method: 1. 使用迎风有限体积法进行空间离散，半隐式有限差分法进行时间离散，对状态问题系统进行离散化；2. 通过离散伴随方案计算离散化成本函数的梯度；3. 使用Adam梯度下降法最小化简化成本泛函

Result: 提出了完整的离散最优控制算法框架，并通过多个数值结果验证了所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: 成功开发了一种针对Keller-Segel趋化系统的最优控制算法，该方法能够有效处理边界和分布控制，数值实验证明了算法的效率和可行性

Abstract: In this work we introduce a new optimal control algorithm for the Keller-Segel chemo-attraction system, where both boundary and distributed controls are considered and both are associated with introducing/removing the amount of chemical substances in the system. The key idea of our approach is to design the optimal control algorithm after discretizing the state problem system, which is done using an upwind finite volume scheme in space and a semi-implicit finite difference in time. Then, the discrete optimal control is approximated identifying the gradient of the reduced discrete cost via the discrete adjoint scheme. Finally, to minimize the reduced cost functional, we use a gradient descent type method (Adam scheme). Moreover, several numerical results are presented to illustrate the efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [125] [A heavy-ball type curve search method for smooth convexly constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19032)
*Federica Donnini,Pierluigi Mansueto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种适用于光滑凸约束优化问题的重球型方法，该方法通过曲线搜索全局化框架确保可行性，在动量更新不可行时平滑回退到可行下降方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Polyak重球等动量技术能加速优化算法，但在约束优化中应用有限，主要挑战在于保持可行性和确保收敛性。需要开发能在约束设置中有效利用动量加速的方法。

Method: 提出重球型方法，结合曲线搜索全局化框架：尝试动量更新，通过曲线搜索强制执行Armijo型下降条件；当动量步不可行或不接受时，平滑回退到可行下降方向。还讨论了非单调曲线搜索、谱步长选择和自适应动量策略等机制。

Result: 证明了算法是良定义的且全局收敛到稳定点；数值实验表明该方法在基准问题上具有鲁棒性，与最先进方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 成功将重球动量技术扩展到约束优化问题，通过曲线搜索框架解决了可行性保持和收敛性保证的挑战，为约束优化中的动量加速提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses smooth convexly constrained optimization problems where the Euclidean projection onto the feasible set is computationally tractable. Although momentum techniques like Polyak's heavy-ball are known for accelerating optimization algorithms, their use in constrained settings remains limited due to challenges in preserving feasibility and ensuring convergence. We thus propose a heavy-ball-type method that extends to the constrained case a recently introduced curve-search globalization framework. The method attempts a momentum update and performs a curvilinear search to enforce an Armijo-type descent condition: when the momentum step is infeasible or unacceptable, the algorithm smoothly reverts to a feasible descent direction. We prove that the algorithm is well-defined and globally convergent to stationary points; the derivation of these results is nontrivial due to the use of a heavy-ball type direction in a constrained setting, where it may generate infeasible iterates. We discuss the incorporation of further mechanisms into the algorithm, including non-monotone curve search, spectral steplength selection and an adaptive momentum strategy. Numerical experiments on benchmark problems show the method is robust and competitive with the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [126] [Preference-Based Optimisation in Group Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19050)
*A. R. M. Wolfert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ODESYS/FIVES方法将多目标优化转化为群体决策，通过统一偏好域和有效偏好函数建模，为复杂设计决策系统提供单一最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标优化方法（如MOO-CP或MIP）在群体决策中失败，因为它们在没有有效偏好基础的情况下聚合异构目标，产生帕累托集而非唯一可执行决策。由于只有人类定义目标，偏好构成决策的合法基础。

Method: 提出ODESYS方法学，采用IMAP求解器，实现集成多目标设计优化和多准则决策。其扩展形式ODESYS/FIVES通过将系统行为映射到统一的偏好-性能域，实现仿射偏好聚合，并保持与已验证ODESYS 1.0结果的等价性。

Result: ODESYS/FIVES为高度约束问题提供单一最优解，保证可行且可接受的结果。两个应用案例展示了将多目标优化转化为纯粹群体决策的能力，在物理社会可达范围内实现共同目的的最佳适配。

Conclusion: 通过建立四个条件（偏好关键、集成、关联、唯一性），ODESYS/FIVES方法学为复杂设计决策系统提供了理论基础和实用框架，将多目标优化问题转化为基于有效偏好建模的群体决策问题，产生单一可执行的最佳适配解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional multi-objective optimisation approaches (e.g., MOO-CP or MIP) fail in group decision-making by aggregating heterogeneous objectives without a valid preference foundation, producing Pareto sets instead of a unique actionable decision. As only humans define objectives, preferences constitute the legitimate basis for decision-making. Accordingly, four conditions for complex design-decision systems are established: (1) Preference-Key - all objectives, constraints, and trade-offs are evaluated within a unified preference domain using valid preference function modelling (PFM); (2) Integration - feasible system performance (object capability) and acceptable actor preferences (subject desirability) coexist within a single design-decision space; (3) Association - actors freely specify individual preferences and weights, enabling consistent aggregation towards group-optimal decision-making; and (4) Uniqueness - the solver identifies a single best-fit solution with maximum aggregated preference.
  The ODESYS methodology, employing the IMAP solver, enables integrated multi-objective design optimisation and multi-criteria decision-making. Its extension within the ODESYS/FIVES formulation broadens applicability while achieving elegant simplicity, explicitly operationalising affine preference aggregation and preserving equivalence with validated ODESYS 1.0 results. By mapping system behaviour into a unified preference-performance domain, ODESYS/FIVES delivers a single best-fit solution, even for highly constrained problems, guaranteeing feasible and acceptable outcomes.
  Two applications demonstrate transformation of multi-objective optimisation into pure group decision-making, achieving a best-fit-for-common-purpose within socio-physical reach.

</details>


### [127] [Stochastic Virtual Power Plant Dispatch via Temporally Aggregated Distributed Predictive Control with Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19106)
*Luca Santosuosso,Fei Teng,Sonja Wogrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种结合MPC、时间序列聚合和分布式优化的新型控制器，解决虚拟电厂在可再生能源、电价和需求不确定性下的能源调度问题，显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机模型预测控制在处理虚拟电厂调度时面临计算不可行性挑战，特别是当调度时域、场景集和资产组合扩大时，非线性动态和多源不确定性使问题变得计算上难以处理。

Method: 提出一种新型控制器，将模型预测控制与时间序列聚合和分布式优化无缝集成，同时减少问题的时间维度、资产维度和场景维度，利用先前MPC迭代的对偶信息自适应优化时间聚合。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的控制器相比传统随机MPC减少50%以上的运行时间，并在全尺度调度模型不可行时恢复可处理性，同时通过理论验证的近似误差界限提供严格的性能保证。

Conclusion: 该控制器成功解决了虚拟电厂在多重不确定性下的计算可扩展性问题，通过多维降维技术实现了计算效率与性能保证的良好平衡，为大规模能源系统调度提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the energy dispatch of a virtual power plant comprising renewable generation, energy storage, and thermal units under uncertainty in renewable output, energy prices, and energy demand. The nonlinear dynamics and multiple sources of uncertainty render traditional stochastic model predictive control (MPC) computationally intractable as the dispatch horizon, scenario set, and asset portfolio expand. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel controller that seamlessly integrates MPC with time series aggregation and distributed optimization, simultaneously reducing the temporal, asset, and scenario dimensions of the problem. The resulting controller provides a rigorous performance guarantee through theoretically validated bounds on its approximation error, while leveraging dual information from previous MPC iterations to adaptively optimize the temporal aggregation. Numerical results show that the proposed controller reduces runtime by over 50% relative to traditional stochastic MPC and, crucially, restores tractability where the full-scale dispatch model proves intractable.

</details>


### [128] [Fast and Effective Computation of Generalized Symmetric Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19147)
*Lei Yang,Han Wan,Min Zhang,Ling Liang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非凸、非光滑、非Lipschitz的广义对称矩阵分解模型，建立了精确惩罚性质和精确松弛关系，并设计了基于平均型非单调交替更新方法（A-NAUM）的求解算法，证明了全局收敛性和收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵分解在机器学习、图像科学、工程等领域有广泛应用，但现有模型往往具有非凸、非光滑、非Lipschitz等复杂特性，且对称性约束难以处理。需要开发能够统一处理这类广义对称矩阵分解问题的理论框架和高效算法。

Method: 1. 建立精确惩罚性质：证明在适当条件下，对称诱导二次惩罚项能够在惩罚参数足够大时精确强制执行对称性；2. 建立精确松弛关系：通过辅助变量分裂将原问题与松弛势函数联系起来；3. 提出A-NAUM算法：基于松弛势函数设计平均型非单调交替更新方法，包含近似最小化因子块和闭式更新辅助块；4. 引入平均型非单调线搜索确保收敛；5. 基于Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质分析收敛性。

Result: 理论方面：证明了精确惩罚性质和精确松弛关系的有效性；算法方面：A-NAUM在温和条件下具有良好的定义性；收敛性方面：基于KL性质证明了整个序列全局收敛到稳定点，并推导了收敛速率；实验方面：在真实数据集上的数值实验验证了A-NAUM的高效性。

Conclusion: 本文为广义对称矩阵分解问题提供了一个统一的理论框架和高效求解算法。所建立的精确性理论为处理对称约束提供了严格基础，A-NAUM算法结合了非单调策略和交替更新机制，在保证收敛的同时提升了实际性能。理论分析和数值实验共同验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a nonconvex, nonsmooth, and non-Lipschitz generalized symmetric matrix factorization model that unifies a broad class of matrix factorization formulations arising in machine learning, image science, engineering, and related areas. We first establish two exactness properties. On the modeling side, we prove an exact penalty property showing that, under suitable conditions, the symmetry-inducing quadratic penalty enforces symmetry whenever the penalty parameter is sufficiently large but finite, thereby exactly recovering the associated symmetric formulation. On the algorithmic side, we introduce an auxiliary-variable splitting formulation and establish an exact relaxation relationship that rigorously links stationary points of the original objective function to those of a relaxed potential function. Building on these exactness properties, we propose an average-type nonmonotone alternating updating method (A-NAUM) based on the relaxed potential function. At each iteration, A-NAUM alternately updates the two factor blocks by (approximately) minimizing the potential function, while the auxiliary block is updated in closed form. To ensure the convergence and enhance practical performance, we further incorporate an average-type nonmonotone line search and show that it is well-defined under mild conditions. Moreover, based on the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property and its associated exponent, we establish global convergence of the entire sequence to a stationary point and derive convergence rate results. Finally, numerical experiments on real datasets demonstrate the efficiency of A-NAUM.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

### [129] [Classifying submodules over monoidal categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18264)
*Hadi Salmasian,Alistair Savage,Yaolong Shen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了幺半范畴上模范畴子模的分类，扩展了Coulembier关于幺半范畴中张量理想分类的思想，建立了子模分类框架并应用于定向结范畴的模块结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Coulembier关于幺半范畴中张量理想分类的工作，研究更一般的模范畴中子模的分类问题，为量子对称对和扭曲反射方程相关结构提供分类理论框架。

Method: 开发了一个适用于配备扭曲柱面扭转结构的模范畴的框架，建立了模范畴子模与路径代数模子模之间的保序双射，分析了该对应在幂等完备化和分拆Grothendieck群下的行为。

Result: 在温和假设下，建立了模范畴子模与路径代数模子模的保序双射，该对应与幂等完备化兼容，并给出了基于不可分解对象的分类判据。应用于非定向结范畴作为定向结范畴的模范畴，完全分类了其子模。

Conclusion: 该工作为配备扭曲柱面扭转的模范畴提供了系统的子模分类理论，将Coulembier的张量理想分类推广到更一般的模块设置，并在结理论中展示了该框架的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study the classification of submodules of module categories over monoidal categories, extending ideas of Coulembier on the classification of tensor ideals in monoidal categories. We develop a framework that applies to module categories equipped with a twisted cylinder twist, a structure closely related to the twisted reflection equation and quantum symmetric pairs. Under mild assumptions, we establish an order-preserving bijection between submodules of a module category $\mathcal{M}$ and submodules of the path-algebra module $\mathcal{M}(1,-)$. We show that this correspondence is compatible with idempotent completion and analyze its behavior under decategorification to the split Grothendieck group, giving criteria for classification in terms of indecomposable objects. As an application, we study the disoriented skein category as a module category over the oriented skein category, describe its indecomposable objects, and obtain a complete classification of its submodules.

</details>


### [130] [Silting theory and derived base change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18790)
*Riku Fushimi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在交换完全局部诺特环上建立倾斜复形与简单心集合之间的双射，并应用于不同环之间的倾斜复形对应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koenig和Yang在域上的有限维代数中建立了倾斜复形与有界导出范畴中简单心集合的双射，但这一结果在更一般的环上尚未建立。本文旨在将这一对应推广到交换完全局部诺特环上。

Method: 在交换完全局部诺特环$(R,\m,k)$上工作，而不是在域上。通过推广Koenig-Yang对应，建立倾斜复形与简单心集合之间的双射。然后将这一结果应用于诺特$R$-代数$\Lambda$和$\Lambda\ten^\LL_RS$之间的倾斜复形对应，其中$(R,\m,k)\to(S,\n,k)$是交换完全局部诺特环的态射。

Result: 成功建立了在交换完全局部诺特环上倾斜复形与简单心集合之间的双射。作为应用，构造了诺特$R$-代数$\Lambda$上的倾斜复形与$\Lambda\ten^\LL_RS$上的倾斜复形之间的双射，推广了诺特代数上倾斜复形的已知结果。

Conclusion: 本文将Koenig-Yang对应从域推广到交换完全局部诺特环，建立了更一般的倾斜复形与简单心集合的双射。这一推广使得能够构造不同环之间的倾斜复形对应，为研究诺特代数上的倾斜理论提供了新的工具。

Abstract: For finite-dimensional algebras over a field, Koenig and Yang established a bijection between silting complexes and simple-minded collections in the bounded derived category, with further contributions by many authors in various settings. In this paper, we work over a commutative complete local noetherian ring $(R,\m,k)$ rather than over a field and establish a bijection in this more general setting.
  As an application of this generalization, we construct a bijection between silting complexes over a noetherian $R$-algebra $Λ$ and silting complexes over $Λ\ten^\LL_RS$ for any morphism of commutative complete local noetherian rings $(R,\m,k)\to(S,\n,k)$. This result generalizes some known results on silting complexes over noetherian algebras.

</details>


### [131] [Pascal, Catalan, Motzkin triangles and tensor product multiplicities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19069)
*L. Poulain d'Andecy*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 论文通过sl_2表示论视角，对Catalan三角形、Motzkin三角形及其推广进行初等讨论，揭示这些整数三角形可由Pascal规则生成，并解释其中的"平方和"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在从表示论角度，特别是sl_2表示论，对Catalan三角形、Motzkin三角形及其推广进行初等讨论。作者希望展示这些整数三角形可以通过经典的Pascal规则从精心选择的初始条件生成，并解释这些三角形中出现的"平方和"现象。

Method: 采用初等方法分析整数三角形，特别是Catalan三角形和Motzkin三角形及其推广。通过sl_2表示论框架，证明这些三角形中的数字可以作为sl_2表示张量积中的重数出现。方法包括：1) 从精心选择的初始条件出发，应用Pascal规则生成三角形；2) 将三角形中的数字表示为广义二项式系数的差；3) 通过表示论透镜分析三角形中的"平方和"现象。

Result: 1) 证明了Catalan三角形、Motzkin三角形及其推广可以通过Pascal规则从特定初始条件生成；2) 展示了这些三角形中的数字是sl_2表示张量积中出现的重数；3) 将这些数字表达为广义二项式系数的差；4) 从表示论角度解释了这些三角形中观察到的"平方和"现象。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了整数三角形（特别是Catalan和Motzkin三角形及其推广）与sl_2表示论之间的深刻联系。通过初等方法，证明了这些三角形可以通过Pascal规则生成，其中的数字对应于表示论中的重数，并且可以表示为广义二项式系数的差。这种表示论视角不仅为理解这些三角形提供了新的框架，也解释了其中观察到的"平方和"现象。

Abstract: The main purpose of this note is to provide an elementary discussion of some simple triangles of integer numbers in particular through their connections with representation theory of $sl_2$. The triangles under consideration are the Catalan triangle and the Motzkin triangle together with their generalisations that we introduce here. We advocate the point of view that these triangles are given by the well-known and classical Pascal rule starting from a well-chosen initial condition. We give an elementary derivation of the fact that the numbers in these triangles are multiplicities appearing in tensor products of $sl_2$-representations and that they are simply expressed as a difference of generalised binomial coefficients. We also take the opportunity to discuss the ``sum of squares'' phenomenon that happens in these triangles through the lense of representation theory.

</details>


### [132] [The variety of Lie algebra representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19123)
*Bruna Mariana Braido da Silva Percinotti*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了n维李代数到实半单李代数表示构成的仿射簇，利用实几何不变量理论构造了自然矩映射和能量泛函，分析了临界点结构并证明了半单对全局最小化轨道能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李代数表示簇的几何结构，特别是通过能量最小化方法理解半单表示的刚性性质，为Mostow定理等经典结果提供新的几何证明。

Method: 采用实几何不变量理论，在GL(n,ℝ)×Inn(𝔤)作用下构造矩映射和能量泛函，分析能量泛函的临界点结构，特别关注半单表示的能量最小化性质。

Result: 证明了半单对（半单李代数的表示）在其轨道中全局最小化能量；获得了半单同态刚性的初等证明；推导了Mostow定理关于半单子代数相容Cartan对合存在性的新证明；获得了高能量临界点结构的相关结果。

Conclusion: 通过能量最小化方法成功建立了李代数表示簇的几何框架，为理解半单表示的刚性提供了新视角，并将经典结果纳入统一的几何框架中。

Abstract: We study the affine variety $L_{n}(\mathfrak{g})$ of Lie algebra representations, the collection of all homomorphisms from an arbitrary $n$-dimensional Lie algebra into a fixed real semi-simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$. Using techniques from real Geometric Invariant Theory, we equip this variety with a natural moment map and associated energy functional arising from the action of the real reductive group $GL(n,\mathbb{R}) \times \text{Inn}(\mathfrak{g})$.
  We analyze the critical points of the energy functional and describe their structure. In particular, we prove that every semi-simple pair, that is representations of semi-simple Lie algebras, will globally minimize the energy in its orbit. As consequences, we obtain an elementary proof of the rigidity of semi-simple homomorphisms and derive a new proof of the Mostow theorem on the existence of compatible Cartan involutions for semi-simple subalgebras. Subsequent results concerning the structure of critical points of higher energy are also obtained.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Borsuk-Ulam type theorem for Stiefel manifolds and orthogonal mass partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18550)
*Oleg R. Musin*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 将Borsuk-Ulam定理推广到Stiefel流形，用于保证在给定m个测度时存在k个相互正交的超平面，其中任意n个超平面将每个测度等分为2^n份


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何保证在给定多个测度的情况下，存在相互正交的超平面系统，使得这些超平面能够将测度等分。这是对经典Borsuk-Ulam定理的推广，旨在解决高维空间中的等分问题。

Method: 将Borsuk-Ulam定理推广到Stiefel流形上，利用拓扑方法分析超平面的正交性和等分性质。通过建立理论框架，推导出保证存在所需超平面系统的d的界。

Result: 得到了保证存在k个相互正交超平面的d的界，其中任意n个超平面将每个测度等分为2^n份。当n=k时，结果与文献[11]的界一致，但增加了超平面相互正交的更强结论。

Conclusion: 成功将Borsuk-Ulam定理推广到Stiefel流形，为高维空间中测度的正交超平面等分问题提供了理论保证，强化了现有结果。

Abstract: A generalization of the Borsuk-Ulam theorem to Stiefel manifolds is considered. This theorem is applied to derive bounds on $d$ that guarantee--for a given set of $m$ measures in $\mathbb{R}^d$--the existence of $k$ mutually orthogonal hyperplanes, any $n$ of which partition each of the measures into $2^n$ equal parts. If $n=k$, the result corresponds to the bound obtained in [11], but with the stronger conclusion that the hyperplanes are mutually orthogonal.

</details>


### [134] [Cohomology of dilute Temperley--Lieb algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18704)
*Andrew Fisher,Daniel Graves*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 证明了稀薄Temperley-Lieb代数的(上)同调在所有正度数上消失


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀薄Temperley-Lieb代数是从统计力学中可解晶格模型研究中产生的Temperley-Lieb代数的变体，研究其同调性质有助于理解这些代数的结构特征

Method: 未在摘要中明确说明具体方法，但论文通过数学证明建立了稀薄Temperley-Lieb代数的同调性质

Result: 证明了稀薄Temperley-Lieb代数的(上)同调在所有正度数上都为零，即这些代数在正度数上没有非平凡的同调群

Conclusion: 稀薄Temperley-Lieb代数具有平凡的正度数同调，这一结果揭示了这些代数变体的代数拓扑性质

Abstract: Dilute Temperley--Lieb algebras are variants of Temperley--Lieb algebras arising in statistical mechanics in the study of solvable lattice models. In this paper we prove that the (co)homology of dilute Temperley--Lieb algebras vanishes in all positive degrees.

</details>


### [135] [On Topological André-Quillen homology of Eilenberg-MacLane spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18932)
*Cyril Barlasov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 计算Eilenberg-MacLane谱（如Hℤ和Hℤ/pⁿ）的拓扑André-Quillen同调，扩展了H𝔽ₚ的结果，采用类似Cartan计算Steenrod代数的方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Dundas、Lindenstrauss和Richter的工作，计算Eilenberg-MacLane谱的拓扑André-Quillen同调，特别是Hℤ和Hℤ/pⁿ的情况。H𝔽ₚ的情况已在Basterra和Mandell的未发表工作中解决，后由Brantner和Mathew在谱划分李代数背景下完善。本文旨在提供更简洁的计算方法。

Method: 采用类似Cartan计算Steenrod代数的方法，基于Dundas、Lindenstrauss和Richter的工作框架，计算拓扑André-Quillen同调。方法相对简洁，并考虑了相对于Hℤ基的计算。

Result: 成功计算了Eilenberg-MacLane谱Hℤ和Hℤ/pⁿ的拓扑André-Quillen同调，提供了相对于Hℤ基的一些计算结果。方法比先前工作更简洁。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种计算Eilenberg-MacLane谱拓扑André-Quillen同调的有效方法，扩展了H𝔽ₚ的结果到Hℤ和Hℤ/pⁿ，方法类似Cartan的Steenrod代数计算，更加简洁。

Abstract: Based on the work of Dundas, Lindenstrauss and Richter we compute the topological André-Quillen homology with reduced coefficients for Eilenberg-MacLane spectra such as $H\mathbb{Z}$ and $H\mathbb{Z}/p^n$. The case of $H\mathbb{F}_p$ was settled in an unpublished work of Basterra and Mandell, which was refined later by Brantner and Mathew in the context of spectral partition Lie algebras. Our approach is similar to Cartan's calculation of the Steenrod algebra, and eventually shorter. We also present some computations relative to the $H\mathbb{Z}$ base.

</details>

### [136] [Genetic determinism of circadian rhythm of feed intake and relation with feed efficiency evaluated in group-housed growing Large White pigs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18081)
*Lucile Riaboff,Ingrid David*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究通过时间-频率分析从电子饲喂站数据中提取了三个反映猪采食昼夜节律的新性状，并评估了这些性状的遗传参数及其与剩余采食量的遗传相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管采食行为的遗传参数已被广泛研究，但群养自由采食生长猪在整个育肥阶段采食量昼夜节律的遗传决定机制从未被研究过。动物昼夜节律与代谢优化之间存在已知关系，因此需要探索采食昼夜节律的遗传基础及其与饲料效率的关系。

Method: 研究对2,297头大白猪的电子饲喂数据进行时间-频率分析，提取了三个反映采食昼夜节律的新性状：DayCR（反映整个育肥期采食昼夜节律的一致性）、IndexCR和gCR（反映节律建立的早熟性）。使用多性状模型估计这些性状的遗传力及其与剩余采食量的遗传相关性。

Result: 三个昼夜节律性状显示出中等遗传力（h²范围：0.24-0.35），剩余采食量具有高遗传力（0.41）。昼夜节律性状间存在高遗传相关性（绝对值范围：0.87-0.98），表明在大多数育肥日（特别是育肥后期）表现出24小时采食周期性的猪更早建立昼夜节律。昼夜节律性状与剩余采食量之间存在低但有利的遗传相关性（绝对值范围：0.18-0.27），表明具有一致且早期24小时采食周期性的动物倾向于更高效利用饲料。

Conclusion: 时间-频率分析可用于检测生长猪育肥期间采食模式的24小时周期性。研究结果表明昼夜节律建立的变异性部分受遗传驱动，支持对昼夜节律性状进行遗传选择的可行性。选择具有早期和一致24小时采食周期性的动物可能促进代谢稳态，从而提高动物性能和抗逆性。

Abstract: Background Genetic parameters of feeding behaviours traits from electronic feeding stations in relation to feed efficiency have been widely explored. However, genetic determinism of the circadian rhythm of feed intake throughout the fattening phase in group-housed growing pigs fed ad libitum has never been investigated, despite the well-known relationships between animals' circadian rhythms and the optimization of their metabolism. The objective of this study was to (i) propose three new traits derived from time-frequency approach applied to electronic feeding data from 2,297 Large White pigs that reflect the consistency of circadian feed intake rhythm throughout fattening (so called DayCR) and the precocity of its establishment (so called IndexCR and gCR), and then to (ii) estimate the heritability of those traits and their genetic correlations with residual feed intake using a multiple trait model. Results Results highlighted moderate heritability estimates for the three circadian traits (range h2: [0.24; 0.35]) and high heritability for residual feed intake (0.41). High genetic correlations (range of absolute values: [0.87; 0.98]) among circadian traits suggested that pigs exhibiting a 24-hour periodicity in feed intake on most days of fattening, particularly during the final fattening period, establish their circadian rhythm earlier than the other pigs. The low (range of absolute values: [0.18; 0.27]) but favourable genetic correlations between residual feed intake and circadian traits revealed that animals with a consistent and early 24-hour periodicity of feed intake also tend to be more feed efficient. Conclusions This study proposed to apply time-frequency analysis on longitudinal feeding data to detect 24-hour periodicities in the hourly feed intake pattern across days throughout fattening in growing-pigs. Results suggested that part of the variability observed in the establishment of circadian rhythm is genetically driven, further supporting the feasibility of genetic selection on circadian traits. Considering the well-established biological mechanisms underlying circadian feeding rhythm, selecting animals for their ability to exhibit an early and consistent 24-hour periodicity of feed intake could promote metabolic homeostasis, thereby enhancing animal performance and resilience.

</details>


### [137] [Interplay between evolutionary and epidemic time scales challenges the outcome of control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18801)
*Santiago Lamata-Otín,Alex Arenas,Jesús Gómez-Gardeñes,David Soriano-Paños*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个考虑病毒进化的SIR模型扩展，发现病毒进化可导致疫情超指数早期增长、产生突发性疫情转变，并削弱控制政策的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SIR模型假设病原体特性固定，忽略了病毒进化对疫情动态的影响。需要建立考虑病毒进化的最小扩展模型，以理解进化如何改变疫情特征和控制策略效果。

Method: 提出了SIR模型的最小扩展，允许传染性进化。推导了控制该行为的临界突变率和干预时间的解析表达式，分析了不同控制策略的对称性。

Result: 病毒进化可导致疫情早期超指数增长、产生突发性疫情转变。过早解除干预措施可能导致比不采取行动更严重的疫情情景。缩短感染期仅阻碍传播而不抑制病毒进化，而降低传播率既能减少病例又能减缓病毒进化。

Conclusion: 病毒进化显著改变疫情动态，削弱传统控制策略效果。控制策略存在强烈不对称性：降低传播率优于仅缩短感染期，因为前者同时减少病例和减缓病毒进化。过早解除干预措施可能适得其反。

Abstract: The SIR model is the cornerstone model for mathematical epidemiology, explaining key epidemic features such as the second-order transition between disease-free and epidemic states, the initial exponential growth of outbreaks or the short-term benefits of control measures. Nonetheless, the classical SIR model assumes that pathogen traits remain fixed, thus neglecting viral evolution. Here we propose a minimal extension of the SIR model, allowing infectiousness to evolve. We show that such evolution can cause superexponential early growth of outbreaks, create abrupt epidemic transitions, and undermine the effectiveness of control policies, as lifting interventions too early can lead to worse epidemic scenarios than no action. We derive analytical expressions for the critical mutation rate and intervention time governing this behavior, and identify a strong asymmetry between control strategies: while shortening the infectious period hinders transmission without suppressing viral evolution, lowering transmission both reduces cases and slows down viral evolution.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>
